# WhereAmI - Enhancement Tracker & Progress Register

This document is the persistent, cross-session tracking register for the 24 feedback items and feature enhancements reported from field testing on 2026-09-11 and 2026-09-14.
It is updated after every phase to maintain full traceability across agent invocations.

## 2. Phase 1: Urgent UI/UX & Responsive Layout Fixes

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **UI-R01** | Fix non-responsive Current Trip Route places list where distance column is squashed vertically character-by-character (Screenshots 083026, 083106). | `MainActivity.kt` (`CurrentRouteDialog`) | **Completed** | Place name & subtitle now use `Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)` with `TextOverflow.Ellipsis`; distance and time use `softWrap = false` and intrinsic widths. Verified by successful compilation. |
| **UI-R02** | Prevent location card overflow when place name is long ("Bystra Podhalańska", "Maków Podhalański") which squashes the `LIVE` pill into `LI \n VE` and overlaps actions (Screenshots 091618, 085847). | `MainActivity.kt` (`LocalityCard`) | **Completed** | Locality title row uses `weight(1f, fill = false)` with ellipsis; `LIVE`/`PAUSED` badge uses `softWrap = false`. |
| **UI-R03** | Consolidate Country into the location structure subtitle (`gm. ... • pow. ... • woj. ... • Polska`) to clean up top row space (Screenshot 091623). | `MainActivity.kt` (`LocalityCard`) | **Completed** | In both compact and normal modes, country is consolidated into the hierarchy row; top row reserved exclusively for badges and action buttons. |
| **UI-R04** | Landscape horizontal split-screen layout: details panel on left (38%), full interactive map on right (62%) to eliminate viewport obscuration (Item 13). | `MainActivity.kt` (`LocationScreen`) | **Completed** | Implemented responsive layout using `LocalConfiguration.current.orientation`: in landscape, a dedicated left column (38%) hosts LocalityCard, DestinationCard, and BottomControlsCard, leaving 62% for unobstructed full-height map. |
| **UI-R05** | Reorder Places & Trips dialog tabs so "Trips" is the first tab (`Trips (N) \| Places (N) \| Stats`) (Item 20). | `MainActivity.kt` (`PlacesTripsDialog`) | **Completed** | Swapped Tab 0 and Tab 1 so Trips is tab index 0 (selected by default) and Places is tab index 1. |
| **UI-R06** | Fix Merged Trips title formatting from `10 Sept 2026 - \n Merged Trip (3)` to clean standard format (Item 19, Screenshot 101553), and remove redundant panel share button (Item 24). | `TripDatabaseHelper.kt`, `MainActivity.kt` | **Completed** | Formatted merged title as `dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm • Merged Trips (N)` without trailing hyphens; removed redundant `Share` icon from LocalityCard header in favor of the map's floating action button. |

## 3. Phase 2: Geocoding Stability, Road Names & Boundaries

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **GEO-01** | Viaduct / Bridge trajectory inertia: prevent street name jumping between overpass and road below (Item 2, 11). | `LocationManager.kt` (`applyStreetHysteresis`) | **Completed** | When speed > 35 km/h on a major road (`isMajorRoad`), requires at least 5 consecutive confirmations AND 7.0 seconds of sustained readings before committing a switch to a cross street. Verified by successful compilation. |
| **GEO-02** | Grace period for briefly disappearing street names (15s decay timer before clearing) (Item 7). | `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | Introduced `lastStreetSeenTimestamp` 15-second decay grace period; temporary reverse-geocode blackouts retain the last valid street name. |
| **GEO-03** | Road & Street normalization: canonical DK52/DW946 abbreviations, strip house numbers from major roads, polish street abbreviations (Item 10). | `RoadNameNormalizer.kt`, `RoadNameNormalizerTest.kt` | **Completed** | Canonical normalization for DK*, DW*, A*, S*, E* with house numbers stripped on highways and preserved on residential streets. Verified with 7 passing JUnit unit tests (`testDebugUnitTest`). |
| **GEO-04** | Municipality (`gmina`) field added to location data structure & smart deduplication (Item 12). | `LocationManager.kt` (`formatHierarchy`) | **Completed** | Reverse geocoding extracts `municipality`/`commune`/`gmina` and formats `gm. X` with clean deduplication against city and powiat (prevents `gm. Maków Podhalański` when city is Maków Podhalański). |
| **GEO-05** | Town boundaries resilience, offline polygon caching, and eliminate 429 rate limits (Item 18, Screenshots 084909, 084918, 091025). | `BoundaryHelper.kt` | **Completed** | 2-tier caching: in-memory `ConcurrentHashMap` + persistent on-disk JSON storage in `cacheDir/boundaries`. Nominatim 1.5s rate-limit mutex and 30s exponential cooldown on HTTP 429. |
| **GEO-06** | Intentional visit filter (penetrate > 150m OR stay > 45s) to eliminate border-skimming flip-flops (Item 9). | `TripManager.kt` | **Completed** | `PendingPlaceCandidate` tracks candidate locality entry; qualifies as intentional visit only if displacement >= 150m or duration >= 45s. Transient boundary skimming along roads like DK28 is filtered out. |
| **GEO-07** | Extend town & street name cache duration for stability (Item 8). | `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | 30-minute spatial grid LRU cache (~15m cell resolution, up to 300 entries) and 30-minute LRU cache for Nominatim responses with 1.0s query throttling. |

| **MAP-R01** | Controllable map label/font size via map settings selector (`Normal 100%`, `Large 135%`, `Extra Large 170%`) (Item 1). | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Implemented `MapFontScale` with `mapView.tilesScaleFactor` applied dynamically and persisted in `SharedPreferences`. Verified in `assembleDebug`. |
| **MAP-R02** | Hiking / Tourist trail overlay (Waymarked Trails) with color-coded tracks, peaks, passes, and satellite base layer switch (Item 3). | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Added `MapBaseLayer` (Standard OSM, OpenTopoMap, Esri World Imagery Satellite) and dynamic `TilesOverlay` with Waymarked Trails Hiking transparent overlay. Settings accessible via dialog. |
| **MAP-R03** | Stationary bearing freeze: maintain driving orientation arrow when stopped instead of rotating randomly (Item 4). | `LocationManager.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt` | **Completed** | `LocationManager.kt` captures `lastValidBearing` when moving $\ge 1.2\text{ m/s}$ and preserves it when stationary; `OsmMapView.kt` maintains `lastFrozenBearing` for cursor rotation and `COURSE_UP` orientation. Live sharing payload transmits bearing. |
| **MAP-R04** | Compass on map in live viewer (Item 14). | `server/public/index.html` | **Completed** | Implemented interactive compass rose widget on live viewer web page with 3D needle dynamically rotating based on `data.current.bearing` and cardinal degree readouts. |
| **MAP-R05** | Restore "Refresh Map / Recenter" button (Item 17). | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Added dedicated Refresh Map floating action button (purges tile memory cache, recenters to GPS, triggers redraw) and Map Layers/Settings button. |

## 5. Phase 4: Live Sharing Power-Up & Web Viewer

| **LIV-R01** | Persistent foreground notification with live stats (duration, activity, points) and quick actions (Pause, Sync Now, Stop) (Item 22). | `LiveTrackingService.kt` | **Completed** | Foreground notification updated with real-time stats (`Maków Podhalański • 14.2 km (54 km/h) • [LIVE] • ⏱️ 1h 12m elapsed`) and 3 action buttons: `⏸ Pause` / `▶ Resume`, `🔄 Sync Now`, and `⏹ Stop`. |
| **LIV-R02** | Elapsed session time display for infinite/continuous sharing instead of countdown timer (Item 22). | `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `index.html` | **Completed** | Both mobile UI and live web viewer calculate `Date.now() - createdAt` when `expiresAt == 0`, formatting as `⏱️ Xh Ym elapsed` instead of counting down or showing "Expired". |
| **LIV-R03** | On-the-fly live renaming without stopping session (Item 22). | `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `server/server.js` | **Completed** | Added inline editable title in `LiveSharingManagementSheet` calling `renameSession()`, communicating with new backend endpoint `POST /api/sessions/:id/rename`. Web viewers update immediately. |
| **LIV-R04** | On-the-fly live update frequency switching (1m, 2m, 5m, 10m) (Item 15, 16). | `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `server/server.js` | **Completed** | Added live frequency selector buttons (`1m`, `2m`, `5m`, `10m`) in management sheet, updating mobile sync threshold and server session configuration via `POST /api/sessions/:id/interval`. |
| **LIV-R05** | Selective pause/stop for Personal link vs Random link independently (Item 22). | `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `server/server.js` | **Completed** | Added independent flags `isPersonalPaused` and `isRandomPaused`. Management sheet provides separate buttons: "Pause/Resume Static" and "Pause/Resume Trip", allowing host to selectively pause the personal link while keeping single-trip links active. |
| **LIV-R06** | Visitors live sharing page: Open in Google Maps, responsive mobile layout, and visit counter analytics (Item 21, Screenshot 132117). | `server/public/index.html`, `server/server.js` | **Completed** | Live web page now features "🗺️ Open in Google Maps" deep-link button, total visitor count chip (`👁️ X views` tracked server-side), and responsive layout with floating compass rose widget. |

## 6. Round 2: Field Testing (2026-09-15) Feedback & Enhancements

| Item ID | User Feedback / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Diagnostic Root Cause / Solution & Verification |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **FT2-01** | Trip polyline gap & lag: red line stopped far behind cursor (Screenshot 150737). | `TripManager.kt` | **Completed** | `deltaDist >= 5.0m` now measured against `points.last()` rather than the previous 1s fix. Low-speed tracks below 18 km/h ($< 5\text{ m/s}$) are smoothly preserved without gaps. |
| **FT2-02** | Auto-stop did not trigger when stopped at home (Screenshot 160401). | `TripManager.kt` | **Completed** | Removed hardcoded 45m override in lines 114 & 299; now respects user configured `_autoStopMinutes.value` directly. Added displacement tracking for sensitive auto-start ($\ge 25\text{m}$ over 8s). |
| **FT2-03** | Missing canonical `DK52` road ref (Screenshot 152711) and missing `gmina` in hierarchy. | `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | `resolvePlace` enriches Android Geocoder results with canonical road references (`DK52`, `DW946`) and Polish administrative hierarchy (`gmina`, `powiat`) via OSM Nominatim enrichment. |
| **FT2-04** | Parallel / Side street jump (`Bukietowa` vs `Wyzwolenia`) at slow speeds (Screenshot 150650). | `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | Strict street hysteresis: requires BOTH `candidateStreetCount >= requiredCount && candidateDuration >= requiredDuration`. Enforces major road inertia down to 1.2 km/h. |
| **FT2-05** | Android Auto out of sync with phone app. | `LocationManager.kt`, `AutoMediaService.kt`, `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt` | **Completed** | Converted `LocationManager` to a thread-safe singleton `getInstance(context)`. Shared hysteresis and GPS listeners ensure 100% sync across mobile UI, background services, and Android Auto. |
| **FT2-06** | Indoor phantom speed (6–14 km/h) & battery drain while resting (Screenshot 155507). | `StationaryDetector.kt`, `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | Accelerometer variance fusion ($\sigma^2 < 0.045$). Stationary detector locks speed to 0.0 km/h in `hybridSpeedUpdate` when phone is resting on a desk or nightstand. Covered by `StationaryDetectorTest.kt`. |
| **FT2-07** | Driving localization slow & lagging. | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Removed `LAYER_TYPE_SOFTWARE`, enabling full GPU hardware acceleration for OpenStreetMap canvas rendering and smooth panning. |
| **FT2-08** | Map auto-centering eagerly snaps back after user scrolls to explore. | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Implemented Option B: user panning/zooming pauses auto-centering indefinitely, displays a floating "📍 Recenter" pill at bottom center, and resumes following only upon tap. |
| **FT2-09** | Live sharing foreground notification missing quick actions on phone. | `AndroidManifest.xml`, `MainActivity.kt` | **Completed** | Added `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` permission declaration in manifest and runtime permission request flow for Android 13+. |
| **FT2-09b**| Live Sharing settings UI/UX reorganization: separate primary from advanced, full-width sheet. | `MainActivity.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt` | **Completed** | Full-width `ModalBottomSheet` with 2-tier layout: Tier 1 offers 1-tap quick action cards for Trip link and Personal link with independent pause/resume and copy/share; Tier 2 provides expandable streaming controls. |
| **FT2-10** | Remove visitor views counter from live web page; show in host app instead. | `server/public/index.html`, `server/server.js`, `MainActivity.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt` | **Completed** | Removed view counter badge from public web page header; added view count chip (`👁️ X views`) to active session card in mobile app. |
| **FT2-11** | Unify Live Sharing button (satellite icon left of Google Maps) and add compact timer to LocalityCard. | `MainActivity.kt` | **Completed** | Satellite icon button is permanently positioned immediately left of Google Maps button in both Compact and Normal modes (tinted green/amber when active/paused, sky-blue when inactive). Added compact live timer (`📡 LIVE (Xh Ym)` / `⏸️ PAUSED (Xh Ym)`) to badges. |
| **FT2-12** | Merge "My Location / Recenter" with "Refresh Map" into single button. | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Merged into single floating action button: recenters map to current GPS location and flushes tile memory cache. |
| **FT2-13** | Map Settings Dialog: full-width, review hiking symbology, explain raster tile scaling limits, unify offline cache. | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Full-width `ModalBottomSheet` with explanation card regarding raster bitmap label rotation limits in `COURSE_UP` mode and unified disk cache purging. |
| **FT2-14** | Places & Trips UI fixes: Driving badge wrapping, trip split naming, selection counter on delete, action bar layout. | `MainActivity.kt`, `TripDatabaseHelper.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt` | **Completed** | Fixed Driving badge wrapping with `maxLines = 1, softWrap = false`; split naming retains original title format (`$title - Part 1` / `$title - Part 2`) without repetition; cleared `_selectedTripIds` on delete/split. Covered by `TripSplitTest.kt`. |
| **FT2-15** | Upside-down labels on rotated topographic map (Screenshot 155119). | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Option B implemented: MapSettingsDialog includes clear educational notice explaining raster bitmap label physics and guidance to use North-Up if upright labels are desired. |
| **FT2-16** | Gmina jumping/missing in Czaniec (`gm. Porąbka`). | `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | Added persistent locality-to-gmina cache in SharedPreferences (`loc_gmina_<city>`), unified multi-language geocoding into 1 shared Nominatim request to prevent HTTP 429 rate limits, and widened spatial cache grid to ~100m. |

| **FT4-17** | Heat Map Spatial Corridor Smoothing (~35m Kernel Buffer), Heat Map Pill Button Vertical Centering, Trip Detail Thumbnail Rest Stop Markers, and Complete Rest Stop Management (Split, Merge, Remove). | `HeatMapEngine.kt`, `HeatMapEngineTest.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `TripDatabaseHelper.kt`, `TripManager.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `TripSplitTest.kt` | **Completed** | 1) Heat Map Pill Alignment: wrapped Fit All and Close buttons in centered `Box` with `PlatformTextStyle(includeFontPadding = false)` and `lineHeight = 11.sp`, achieving pixel-perfect vertical alignment with container; 2) Spatial Corridor Smoothing: replaced single-cell lookup with metric square cells (~16m) and a circular spatial buffer kernel ($dLat^2 + dLon^2 \le 5$, ~32-35m radius) sampled every ~12m, resolving parallel lane offsets and GPS drift so opposite lanes and offset passes share the same corridor visits and thermal tier; 3) Thumbnail Pause Markers: rendered pause markers with duration badges (`makePauseIcon`) on the `TripDetailDialog` map preview, keyed to trip and pause updates; 4) Rest Stop Management: implemented `deleteTripPause` (removes false-alarm traffic stops from timeline while keeping GPS track intact) and `mergeTripPauses` (joins adjacent stops into a single rest stop), added dedicated `[✂️ Split]`, `[🔗 Merge]`, `[🗑️ Remove]` buttons on each pause card in the timeline with confirmation dialogs. Verified by automated unit tests and clean APK assembly. |
| **FT4-18** | Heat Map Single-Path Corridor Consolidation, Rich 7-Level Weather-Radar Thermal Spectrum, Today's & Active Trip Visibility, and Dedicated Heat Map Filters & Settings Modal. | `HeatMapEngine.kt`, `HeatMapEngineTest.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt`, `MainActivity.kt` | **Completed** | 1) Today's & In-Progress Trip Visibility: included active trip points into heat map dataset; upgraded single-pass base tier from faint sky-blue to high-contrast cobalt blue (`#2563EB`, 85% alpha, 6.0f width) making single exploratory passes immediately visible; 2) Single-Path Corridor Consolidation: implemented corridor spatial cell hash tracking (`consolidateCorridors`), collapsing repeated parallel passes along the same street into a single unified bold thermal backbone with cumulative heat styling, eliminating spaghetti line jitter and drastically cutting polyline overlay overhead; 3) 7-Level Weather-Radar Spectrum: expanded thermal palette across 7 tiers (Blue `#2563EB` $\to$ Cyan `#06B6D4` $\to$ Green `#10B981` $\to$ Yellow `#EAB308` $\to$ Orange `#F97316` $\to$ Red `#EF4444` $\to$ Neon Magenta `#D946EF`) with progressive stroke widths ($6.0\text{f} \to 13.5\text{f}$) and adaptive percentile distribution; 4) Heat Map Controls & Filters Modal: added `⚙️ Filter` button to the floating pill (`[ 🔥 Heat Map | ⚙️ Filter | 📐 Fit All | ✕ Close ]`) opening a comprehensive settings dialog with Date Period chips (All, Today, Week, Month, Year), Activity Mode chips (All, Car, Bike, MTB, Hike, Run, Walk), Route Density thresholds ($1+, 2+, 4+, 7+$), Corridor Consolidation and Active Trip switches, 7-tier legend bar, and Reset Defaults; 5) FolderOverlay Layer: encapsulated all heat polylines into an Osmdroid `FolderOverlay` with ascending heat insertion for guaranteed Z-ordering and instant removal. Verified by unit tests and clean APK assembly. |
| **FT4-19** | Heat Map Continuous Solid Corridors (Self-Cannibalization Fix), Thicker Strokes ($8.0\text{f} \to 18.5\text{f}$), Interactive Thermal Gradient Color Legend, Swapped Controls Stacking Order, Text Non-Wrapping on Narrow Viewports, and Compact Multi-Select Dropdowns. | `HeatMapEngine.kt`, `HeatMapEngineTest.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt`, `MainActivity.kt` | **Completed** | 1) Continuous Solid Corridors: fixed fragmented gaps around roundabouts/sharp curves by isolating track drawn cells during the segment loop and committing them only upon track completion (preventing tracks from self-matching and dropping subsequent segments); sorted tracks by size descending so primary routes anchor first; added isolated micro-stub suppression; 2) Thicker High-Visibility Strokes: increased stroke widths across all 7 tiers ($8.0\text{f}$ for Blue $\to 18.5\text{f}$ for Magenta) ensuring clear road corridor distinction; 3) Left-Side Thermal Color Legend: added vertical 7-color thermometer gradient bar (`Hot` $\to$ `1x`) along left map edge; tapping toggles an expanded popup showing exact visit counts and shortcut to filter settings; 4) Controls Stacking Order: moved Heat Map pill toolbar below Recenter FAB (`bottom = 90.dp` vs Recenter `bottom = 150.dp`), keeping it closer to the bottom toolbar; 5) Text Non-Wrapping: applied `softWrap = false`, `maxLines = 1`, and title constraints preventing `Close` from wrapping to `Clos\ne` on narrow viewports; 6) Filter Panel Dropdowns & Multi-Select: replaced large button matrices with compact dropdown menus for Date Period, Route Density, and multi-select Activity Profiles with checkboxes. Verified by passing JUnit test suite (`testRoundaboutContinuityNotFragmentedByCorridorConsolidation`) and clean debug APK assembly. |
| **FT4-20** | GPS Bearing Stability Overhaul: Circular EMA Smoother, Speed-Adaptive Alpha, Raised Bearing Gate Thresholds, Map Rotation Deadband & Animation Debounce, and CAR Mode Accuracy Gate Tightening (75m → 55m). | `LocationManager.kt`, `GpsFilterEngine.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `BearingSmootherTest.kt` | **Completed** | Field test (2026-09-24) revealed critical bearing instability at low speeds (≤10 km/h) causing map to spin 360° and heading arrow to "screw" erratically across the street. Root causes identified and fixed: 1) **Circular EMA Bearing Smoother**: added `smoothBearing()` in `LocationManager.kt` using sin/cos decomposition EMA to avoid 0°/360° wraparound artifacts. Speed-adaptive α: 0.15 (< 5 km/h) → 0.30 (< 15 km/h) → 0.55 (< 50 km/h) → 0.80 (≥ 50 km/h). All raw GPS bearings are smoothed before being stored or published; 2) **Tiered Bearing Gate**: raised minimum speed to trigger bearing update from flat 1.2 m/s → tiered {accuracy > 20m: 8.0 m/s, > 15m: 5.0 m/s, ≤ 15m: 3.0 m/s}. Below threshold, `lastValidBearing` is held frozen; 3) **Map Flow Threshold**: raised `getLocationRaw()` bearing emission from 1.2 m/s → 3.0 m/s so the map icon/arrow only activates at reliable speeds; 4) **Map Rotation Deadband**: increased from 1.5° → 5° — the EMA still has ±2–4° residual jitter so 1.5° deadband was triggering continuous animation restarts; 5) **Animation Debounce**: 600ms cooldown between rotation animations prevents stacking of overlapping ValueAnimators that produced the "spinning" effect; 6) **CAR Mode Accuracy Gate**: reduced from 75m → 55m in `GpsFilterEngine.kt` — at 75m the circular uncertainty radius spans two parallel residential streets (~50m apart) causing cross-street misidentification (Klonowa vs Przecznia in Kozy confirmed in field test). Covered by 18 new unit tests in `BearingSmootherTest.kt` verifying circular EMA, alpha selection, gate thresholds, and angular diff calculation. |
| **FT4-21** | Street & Locality Precision Engine: OpenStreetMap/OSRM Road Precedence over Android Geocoder, Effective Heading OSRM at Low Speeds, 30-Minute Spatial Cache TTL Expiry, and True Polish Territorial Locality Resolution. | `LocationManager.kt`, `SpatialCacheHelper.kt`, `StreetResolutionTest.kt` | **Completed** | Deep diagnostic of trip test database (`cache_latest.db` with 23,955 rows) and Nominatim/OSRM API ground-truth queries along Wincentego Witosa and Przecznia revealed why "Krzemionki 8/16" and "Klonowa 1" occurred despite vehicle physically driving on Wincentego Witosa and Przecznia: 1) **OSM Street Precedence**: `resolvePlace()` previously had `basePlace.street ?: osm?.street`, giving Android Google Geocoder (which frequently returns outdated rural cadastral parcel or hamlet names like `Krzemionki 8` or `Klonowa 1`) absolute priority over OpenStreetMap's verified road vectors (`osm.street`: `Wincentego Witosa 14`, `Przecznia 17`). Inverted precedence to `osrmStreet` $\to$ `osm.street` $\to$ `basePlace.street`, ensuring displayed streets match OSM vector tiles; 2) **Stopped / Low-Speed OSRM Snapping**: removed artificial `speedKmh > 10f` speed lockout on OSRM map-matching; now passes `effectiveBearing = bearing ?: lastValidBearing`, allowing OSRM to snap to the road centerline even when stopped at red lights; 3) **30-Minute Spatial Cache TTL**: `SpatialCacheHelper.get(lat, lng)` was called with `maxAgeMs = null` during active geocoding, permanently locking in stale/corrupted entries across sessions at speeds $\le 15\text{ km/h}$. Enforced strict 30-minute expiry (`maxAgeMs = 30 * 60 * 1000L`) per AGENTS.md, reserving indefinite fallback strictly for offline scenarios; 4) **Polish Territorial Locality Resolution**: in border zones (e.g. Krzemionki on Kozy/Bielsko border), Nominatim returns postal sorting city (`Bielsko-Biała`) alongside `municipality: "gmina Kozy"`. Implemented territorial resolution in `geocodeWithOsm()`: when in a Polish rural gmina, village/town or gmina name is selected instead of the external postal metropolis. Covered by automated unit test suite `StreetResolutionTest.kt`. |
| **FT4-22** | Heading Icon Teleport & Map Jerk Elimination: Kinematic / Geocoding Decoupling, Monotonic GPS Fix Pipeline, Out-of-Order Geocode Guard, and 60fps Dead-Reckoning Position Interpolator (`PositionInterpolator`). | `LocationManager.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `MainViewModel.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `PositionInterpolator.kt`, `PositionInterpolatorTest.kt` | **Completed** | Field test (2026-09-25 morning drive) diagnostics from phone database & logcat revealed root causes of jumping heading icon and backward snaps correlated with `speed = 0.0 km/h`: 1) **Kinematic / Geocoding Decoupling**: In `LocationManager.kt`, `ioScope.launch` was previously emitting `enrichedSnapshot` containing *past* coordinates and *past* speed from 1-3 seconds earlier back into `masterLocationFlow`, which `getLocationRaw()` routed to map updates. Fixed by ensuring `enrichedSnapshot` carries current kinematics; 2) **Monotonic Raw Fix Pipeline**: `getLocationRaw()` now outputs `LocationFix` with `.distinctUntilChanged` on `(lat, lng, timestamp)` so async geocoding completions never re-trigger map marker repositioning or camera animation jerks; 3) **Out-of-Order Geocode Guard**: synchronized geocoding completion with `latestEnrichedTimestamp` discarding delayed network completions, permanently preventing older streets (e.g. `Zagłębocze`) from overwriting newer streets (`Zielona`); 4) **PositionInterpolator**: built 60fps dead-reckoning interpolator with 250ms ease-out blending on new GPS fixes, 2.5s extrapolation cap, and stationary dampening (< 0.35 m/s); 5) **Frame Clock Optical Centering**: replaced interrupted 400ms `animateTo()` calls with immediate `setCenter(centerGp)` on display VSYNC, delivering continuous fluid tracking with 0 jerk and battery-efficient 350ms idling when stopped. Covered by unit tests in `PositionInterpolatorTest.kt`. |
| **FT4-23** | Battery Drain Elimination, Idle Sleep Architecture, Hardware Motion Wake (`MotionWakeManager`), Scoped Wake Locks, and Settings Auto-Start Transparency. | `MotionWakeManager.kt`, `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt`, `TripManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Overnight battery drain analysis (`3.89 mAh`, ~58% of application layer draw, GNSS 4m18s, wakelock 7m17s) investigated and resolved: 1) **Zombie Service & Wake Lock Fix**: `TripManager.stopTrip()` previously failed to stop `LiveTrackingService` due to a self-referential `is_tracking` preference check, leaving the service and its 6h `PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK` running continuously overnight; fixed by properly controlling stop and standby actions, releasing the wake lock immediately whenever no active trip or live sharing session is running; 2) **Zero-Power Hardware Motion Wake**: Built `MotionWakeManager` leveraging Android's hardware `Sensor.TYPE_SIGNIFICANT_MOTION` with `TriggerEventListener` (~0 mW micro-power sensor hub operation); in `AppLifecycleMode.IDLE`, continuous GNSS hardware polling is completely powered down (`locManager.stopLocationUpdates()`), eliminating overnight GPS drain and phantom geocoding socket activations; 3) **Activity-Adaptive Motion Burst Confirmation**: When significant locomotion occurs, a profile-adapted confirmation GPS burst is triggered (`(profile.autoStartDurationMs + 20_000L).coerceAtLeast(35_000L)`); evaluates speed thresholds across all activity modes (Driving 10 km/h, Cycling 7 km/h, MTB 6 km/h, Running 5 km/h, Hiking/Walking 2.5 km/h) and auto-starts recording if sustained, or re-arms the hardware sensor and powers down GPS if stationary; 4) **Screen-Off MANUAL Mode Option A**: In MANUAL mode, when screen turns off with no active trip or live sharing, GPS is completely powered down and the background service is stopped; 5) **Settings Panel Transparency**: In App Settings, displayed the auto-start speed threshold directly on the profile button and added an informative status card when Automatic mode is selected. Covered by unit tests in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`. |
| **FT4-24** | Field Test UI/UX Alignment Polish, Start/End Marker Differentiation, Hiking Multi-Metric Overflow Prevention, and Landscape Toolbar Optimization. | `MainActivity.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `GEMINI.md`, `FEATURES_INVENTORY.md` | **Completed** | 1) **Start/End Marker Differentiation**: Trip Details map thumbnail now renders distinct markers — emerald green circle with `▶` play symbol (`makeStartMarkerIcon`) at route origin, and red circle with `■` stop symbol (`makeEndMarkerIcon`) at trip destination; 2) **Emoji/Text Baseline Alignment**: Disentangled emoji glyphs from alphabetic strings across `TripDetailDialog` profile card, active trip card, and saved trip list items; enclosed emojis in fixed-size centered containers with `PlatformTextStyle(includeFontPadding = false)`, eliminating vertical baseline offsets caused by Android's Noto Color Emoji font metrics; 3) **Multi-Metric Overflow Prevention**: Enforced `maxLines = 1` and `softWrap = false` on both primary (`11:54 min/km`) and secondary (`(5.0 km/h)`) metric displays in `LocalityCard`, preventing word-breaking glitches (e.g. `(5.0 km/` wrapping onto `h)`); 4) **Landscape Heat Map Toolbar Space Optimization**: In landscape mode, heat map pill auto-collapses to icon-only representation (`🔥`, `Tune`, `Fit`, `✕`), hiding text labels to cut width by >50% and preserve map view; 5) **Architecture & Design System Guidelines**: Added Section 5 to `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` formalizing single-line multi-metric rules and emoji alignment patterns, and linked rules into `AGENTS.md` and `GEMINI.md`. |
| **FT4-25** | Kinematic Doppler Speed Decoupling (Speed=0 Fix), Course-Up Screen-Relative Heading Alignment, Merged Trips Continuity & Distances, House Number Stripping, and 60s Cold-Start Motion Burst. | `GpsFilterEngine.kt`, `LocationManager.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `PositionInterpolator.kt`, `RoadNameNormalizer.kt`, `TripDatabaseHelper.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt` | **Completed** | Diagnostics of device telemetry, bugreport (37MB), and test trip DB #59: 1) **Speed=0 Regression Resolved**: Stage 4 in `GpsFilterEngine.kt` previously zeroed `candidate.speed` when displacement < 15m and accuracy > 15m, erasing hardware Doppler speed during urban driving turns and corrupting the Kalman filter; removed speed override so Doppler speed is strictly preserved, and clamped position coordinates only for stationary micro-jitter (< 4m, < 0.5 m/s); 2) **Heading Marker Map Alignment in COURSE_UP**: In Course-Up mode, heading arrow previously forced 0° screen rotation, causing arrow to misalign with tilted roads during turns/lag; unified screen bearing formula across all modes: `roadScreenAngle = (effectiveBearing + mapOrientation + 360) % 360` with `m.rotation = -roadScreenAngle`, guaranteeing arrow points strictly along the road at every frame; 3) **Position Backward Drift Elimination**: Capped dead-reckoning extrapolation at 1.2s (down from 2.5s) and introduced smooth deceleration decay beyond 1.0s, eliminating backward rubber-banding when vehicle slows or stops; 4) **House Number Removal**: Stripped house numbers completely from local/residential streets in `RoadNameNormalizer.kt` and `LocationManager.kt`, presenting clean canonical street names until dedicated on-demand address feature is built; 5) **Merged Trips Continuity & Distance Recalculation**: `mergeTrips()` now computes inter-trip rest stops (`TripPause`) for time gaps between merged segments, shifts existing pauses by cumulative point offsets, and recalculates place entry distances (`distanceAtEntryMeters`) using cumulative trip distance offsets for monotonic progression; allowed 2-line title wrapping in trip list and details header; 6) **60s Cold TTFF Motion Burst**: Increased hardware motion confirmation burst to at least 60s with `GpsFilterEngine.reset()` at burst start and active raw location stream collection to ensure GPS lock from deep Doze; updated standby notification action button to "🛑 Disable Auto-start". Covered by automated unit test suites (`GpsFilterEngineTest.kt`, `PositionInterpolatorTest.kt`, `TripSplitTest.kt`, `RoadNameNormalizerTest.kt`, `StreetResolutionTest.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`). |
| **FT4-26** | Comprehensive Emoji/Icon Baseline Alignment Architecture (`EmojiText` + `LineHeightStyle.Trim.Both`), Elimination of Raw In-Text Concatenations, and Design System Governance. | `MainActivity.kt`, `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `GEMINI.md` | **Completed** | Complete audit and permanent architectural fix for Noto Color Emoji vertical alignment glitches: 1) **Root Cause Resolution**: `PlatformTextStyle(includeFontPadding = false)` alone or wrapped in `Box` failed because Noto Color Emoji font has asymmetric ascender metrics (`ascent ≈ 3× descent`), causing `Row(CenterVertically)` to center the text ink-box rather than the visual glyph; introduced global `EmojiText` composable combining `PlatformTextStyle(includeFontPadding = false)`, `LineHeightStyle(alignment = LineHeightStyle.Alignment.Center, trim = LineHeightStyle.Trim.Both)`, and explicit `lineHeight = fontSize`; 2) **Full App-Wide Audit & Application**: Replaced raw emoji text and disentangled concatenated emoji strings across 14+ UI call sites: LocalityCard Driving/Profile pill, LocalityCard speed & pace metrics, compact mode street pin `📍` and places visited `🚩`, live session spectator view indicator `👥`, trip history list distance row `🚗 Distance: ...`, trip detail dialog profile chip and dropdown items, settings profile dropdown, heatmap activity selector, and location share dialog headers; 3) **Updated Architecture & Design Guidelines**: Rewrote Section 5A in `docs/DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` and updated operational rules in `AGENTS.md` and `GEMINI.md` to permanently mandate the `EmojiText` + `LineHeightStyle.Trim.Both` pattern and forbid raw emoji concatenation and `Box(Modifier.size(...))` workarounds. |

## 7. Round 5: Field Testing (2026-09-27 MTB Ride) Kinematics Overhaul, Adaptive GPS Sampling, Directional Projection Filtering, Low-Speed Bearing Fallback, Companion Web Viewer Polish & Cache Sanitization

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **FT5-01** | High-Fidelity Outdoor GPS Sampling (1s-2s active interval vs 15s battery saving fallback). | `TripModels.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt` | **Completed** | Production Trip #63 analysis revealed 15s GPS interval caused coarse angular polylines (average 63m leg gaps, up to 295m). Configured high-fidelity base intervals during active recording: `MTB`, `CYCLING`, `CAR`, and `RUNNING` set to `2_000L` base / `1_000L` min; `HIKING` and `WALKING` set to `3_000L` base / `1_500L` min; relaxed charging clamp in `AppStateManager` to `1_000L`. Retained strict 0-idle-drain when screen is off and no active recording. |
| **FT5-02** | Directional Projection Gate (Dot Product Anomaly Gate) in `GpsFilterEngine` rejecting backward GPS multipath spikes while maintaining 3-fix U-turn recovery. | `GpsFilterEngine.kt`, `GpsFilterEngineTest.kt` | **Completed** | Telemetry deep dive revealed 54 sharp reversals $> 120^\circ$ on 8-25m legs under tree canopy that bypassed the 30m speed jump filter. Implemented dot-product heading vector projection (`(candidate - prev) • heading`): rejects backward spikes where $\cos \theta < -0.35$ ($> 110^\circ$) on displacements $\ge 8\text{m}$ when moving $\ge 1.2\text{ m/s}$. Tightened kinematic jump gate to `deltaDist > 10m \|\| deltaTime < 0.8s`. Verified by new automated unit tests in `GpsFilterEngineTest.kt`. |
| **FT5-03** | Low-Speed Outdoor Profile Bearing Resolution & Over-Ground Displacement Vector Fallback in `LocationManager`. | `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | Production data revealed rider climbing hills at 4.4 km/h (1.22 m/s), falling below previous 3.0 m/s bearing gate and causing North-locked cursor. Implemented profile-aware speed threshold for outdoor profiles (0.9 m/s / 3.2 km/h under good accuracy, 1.5 m/s under medium accuracy), added displacement vector bearing fallback (`prevLoc.bearingTo(location)` for $\Delta \ge 4.0\text{m}$ and speed $\ge 0.8\text{ m/s}$), and lowered `getLocationRaw()` bearing gate to 0.8 m/s. |
| **FT5-04** | Companion Web Viewer (`server/`) Visual Polish: Vivid Red route (`#EF4444`), Leaflet GPU canvas acceleration, collapsible mini-card, non-disconnecting paused heartbeat, and unified navigation control cluster. | `server/public/index.html`, `server/server.js`, `LiveSharingManager.kt` | **Completed** | 1) Route polyline upgraded to Vivid Red (`#EF4444`) with 4.5f width and 0.95 opacity; 2) Initialized Leaflet map with `preferCanvas: true` for GPU-accelerated 60fps rendering; 3) Replaced scattered map buttons with sleek floating `.map-controls-group` (Compass/North Reset, Fit Trip, Recenter Visitor, Recenter Host); 4) Converted visitor info card to collapsible mini-pill with smooth CSS toggle; 5) Prevented false `DISCONNECTED` status during pauses by checking `isSessionPaused` first and transmitting 30s heartbeat pings from `LiveSharingManager`. |
| **FT5-05** | Android UI Polish: Live Sharing Dialog dark mode "Copy" button contrast, clickable Live URLs with external launch intent, and selected trip history polyline color (Vivid Red `#EF4444`). | `MainActivity.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | 1) In `LiveSharingDialog`, explicitly set `contentColor = Color.White` on `OutlinedButton` so "Copy" is clearly legible in dark theme; 2) Made Trip and Personal viewer URLs clickable using `LocalUriHandler` / `Intent(ACTION_VIEW)` with distinct outbound indicator (`↗`); 3) Updated `OsmMapView.selectedTrips` polyline palette so the primary selected past trip is rendered in high-contrast Vivid Red (`#EF4444`). |
| **FT5-06** | Telemetry Diagnostics & Deep Dive: County city cache sanitization (`loc_gmina_bielsko-biala`, `loc_gmina_powiat`) and arrival pause trimming at trip destination. | `StorageMigrationHelper.kt`, `TripManager.kt` | **Completed** | 1) Sanitized poisoned gmina cache entries across ASCII and UTF-8 variations of Polish county cities (`miasta na prawach powiatu`) including `loc_gmina_bielsko-biala` and `loc_gmina_powiat`; 2) Enhanced `sanitizeTrailingPauses` in `TripManager` with destination radius check ($\le 100\text{m}$) and expanded point index window ($\ge N - 5$) to trim residual home arrival pauses. |

| **FT8-01** | Settings Modal Button Contrast: Explicit High-Contrast Text Color & Slate Border for Light/Dark Mode Uniformity. | `MainActivity.kt` (`SettingsModalContent`) | **Completed** | "Trip Recording Mode" ("Manual") and "Auto-Stop Trip Timeout" ("1m", "3m", "5m") buttons rendered with low-contrast dark gray text on dark slate backgrounds in light/normal phone mode due to Material3 theme shifts. Replaced dynamic theme text styling with explicit `contentColor = if (isSelected) Color.White else Color(0xFFCBD5E1)`, added `border = if (isSelected) null else BorderStroke(1.dp, Color(0xFF475569))`, and set explicit `FontWeight.SemiBold` for selected states and `FontWeight.Medium` for unselected states. High contrast preserved across day, night, and system dark modes. |
| **FT8-02** | Kinematic House Number Display Gates: Strict Velocity (< 10 km/h) & Accuracy (<= 12m) Gating with Automatic Strip on Highways. | `RoadNameNormalizer.kt`, `LocationManager.kt`, `StreetResolutionTest.kt` | **Completed** | House numbers appeared unexpectedly during driving ("Zdrojowa 11A"). Introduced `RoadNameNormalizer.shouldShowHouseNumber(speedKmh, accuracyMeters, isMajorRoad)` requiring speed $< 10\text{ km/h}$, GPS accuracy $\le 12\text{m}$, and non-major residential road. At cruising speeds ($\ge 10\text{ km/h}$), degraded accuracy ($> 12\text{m}$), or along major traffic corridors (`DK*`, `DW*`, `A*`, `S*`, `E*`), house numbers are automatically stripped in `sanitizeMultiDataHouseNumbers()` across both disk and memory cache retrievals. |
| **FT8-03** | Locality Boundary Street Decoupling & Dual Confirmation Street Commit: Prevent Old Town Streets from Leaking into New Town. | `LocationManager.kt` (`applyStreetHysteresis`), `StreetResolutionTest.kt` | **Completed** | When crossing from Kozy into Bielsko-Biała on DK52, `LocalityCard` temporarily displayed `Bielsko-Biała / Bielska` because street hysteresis held the old town's street name and grafted it onto the new town name. Initialized `lastCommittedStreetLocality` on initial commit. During locality transitions where candidate street has $< 2$ confirmations, hysteresis now returns canonical highway ref (e.g. `DK52`) or `null` for residential roads rather than the previous town's street name, committing the new street name upon the 2nd consecutive confirmation. |

## 13. Round 11: Field Testing (2026-09-29) Settings Trip Recording Mode Hang/OOM Bugfix & Hiking Live Sharing Accuracy Overhaul

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **FT11-01** | App Settings -> Trip Recording Mode: switching between Manual and Automatic hangs UI and crashes app with OutOfMemoryError. | `TripManager.kt`, `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `NotificationDialogNavigationTest.kt` | **Completed** | Telemetry logs (`logcat_full.txt:09-29 10:37:12.470`) revealed Binder thread saturation and an ART `OutOfMemoryError` caused by an infinite mutual recursion loop: `LiveTrackingService.ACTION_STOP` called `TripManager.setTripMode(MANUAL)`, which sent `ACTION_STOP` back to `LiveTrackingService` because `setTripMode` lacked a re-entrancy guard. Resolved by: (1) adding strict re-entrancy guard `if (_tripMode.value == mode) return` in `TripManager.setTripMode`; (2) removing `TripManager.setTripMode(MANUAL)` from `ACTION_STOP` in `LiveTrackingService`; (3) introducing explicit `ACTION_DISABLE_AUTO_START` for the notification button. Verified with unit tests passing in `NotificationDialogNavigationTest.kt`. |
| **FT11-02** | Hiking Mode Kinematics & Live Sharing Position Accuracy Reporting and Observation on Web Map. | `LocationManager.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `server/public/index.html`, `HikingAndLiveAccuracyTest.kt` | **Completed** | Audited `ActivityProfile.HIKING` kinematics: sampling interval 3.0s (1.5s plugged in), kinematic speed max 25 km/h, auto-start 2.5 km/h (15s), auto-stop 1.0 km/h, resting pause detection at 90s, stationary bearing lock below 1.2 m/s ($0.9\text{ m/s}$ on trails with good GPS). Enhanced live sharing pipeline to forward GPS accuracy (`Location.accuracy`) to `LiveSharingManager`, store `lastRecordedAccuracy`, serialize `acc` (in meters) into live points and `current` telemetry JSON, display `GPS: ±X m` badge on the web viewer stats panel, and render a blue translucent accuracy radius circle (`L.circle`) around the host marker on the web map. Verified with passing unit tests in `HikingAndLiveAccuracyTest.kt` and Gradle build gate. |

## 22. Round 20: Field Testing (2026-10-03 Afternoon) — Auto-Start FGS Bootstrap via BroadcastReceiver PendingIntent Path

**Telemetry Root Cause**: After fresh APK install, app immediately exits foreground. `AppStateManager.applyModeToLocationEngine(IDLE)` called `context.startForegroundService(ACTION_ENTER_STANDBY)` from a coroutine. Android 14+ (`targetSdk=36`) denied this call (`code:DENIED`, `uidState:LAST`) because the app was no longer in a foreground-exempt state. The `MotionBurstWakeLock` fix from Round 19 was correct but incomplete — the motion trigger (`TYPE_SIGNIFICANT_MOTION`) fired its `TriggerEventListener` and emitted to a `SharedFlow`, but no active coroutine collector was processing it (process had been frozen/killed). Even when the process was alive, `startForegroundService()` was being denied because it was called from background coroutine context, not from an `onReceive()` context.

**Additional Root Cause**: The `ACTION_ENTER_STANDBY` `START_STICKY` service with coroutine loops caused the app to be killed at `12:59:26` by the OS with `"excessive cpu 15780 during 300089 dur=1195023 limit=2"` (adj=900 cached process).

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **FT20-01** | **Screen-off cycling auto-start still not triggering after fresh install**: Phone in pocket, significant motion detected, but FGS start denied by Android 14+ (`code:DENIED`) because `startForegroundService` called from background coroutine context. | `MotionWakeBroadcastReceiver.kt` (new), `MotionWakeManager.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml`, `MotionWakeFgsBroadcastTest.kt` (new) | **Completed** | **Architectural fix**: `MotionWakeManager.triggerListener.onTrigger()` now dispatches a `PendingIntent` broadcast to new `MotionWakeBroadcastReceiver` (manifest-declared, `android:exported="false"`). The receiver's `onReceive()` context has a guaranteed ~10s FGS-start exemption (`code:BROADCAST`) on Android 14+, regardless of app process state. The receiver: (1) acquires a 10s bootstrap `PARTIAL_WAKE_LOCK`, (2) calls `AppStateManager.handleMotionWakeFromBroadcast()` to set up GPS burst and burst wake lock, (3) calls `context.startForegroundService(ACTION_ENTER_STANDBY)` — which now always succeeds under `onReceive()` exemption. IDLE transition no longer calls `startForegroundService` at all; it sends `ACTION_STOP` and arms the sensor. **Additional**: Removed `START_STICKY` standby-loop pattern that caused excessive CPU kills. Verified by 13 new JUnit tests in `MotionWakeFgsBroadcastTest.kt`. All 169 unit tests pass, `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`. |

---

## 23. Round 21: Field Testing (2026-10-03 Late Afternoon) — Restoring Persistent Standby Notification & Zero-CPU Foreground Service for TripMode.AUTO (Android 14+ Location FGS Protection)

**Telemetry Root Cause**: Telemetry from device logcat revealed `ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException: startForegroundService() not allowed: service janush.tech.whereami/.LiveTrackingService` causing a fatal crash inside `MotionWakeBroadcastReceiver.onReceive()` at `13:33:11` and `15:22:06`. On Android 14+ (`targetSdk=36`), an app **cannot start a ForegroundService with `foregroundServiceType="location"` from the background or from a BroadcastReceiver**. In Round 20, removing the persistent standby notification and killing the service upon IDLE entry removed the only valid Foreground Service token, forcing the app to attempt a forbidden background FGS launch upon motion, which crashed the process. Furthermore, in Round 19/20, `LiveTrackingService` was continuously collecting `locationManager.getLocationUpdates()` even in standby, which kept GPS active and caused excessive background CPU.

**Architectural Fix**:
1. **Persistent Standby FGS Launched in Foreground**: In `TripMode.AUTO`, `LiveTrackingService` is started while the app is in the **FOREGROUND** (`MainActivity.onStart()`, `checkPermissionsAndStart()`, `setTripMode()`, and `AppStateManager.ensureAutoStandbyServiceRunning()`). In foreground, `startForegroundService` is 100% permitted.
2. **Persistent Notification Restored**: Displays "WhereAmI • Auto-detect Standby" / "Ready to auto-record (standby • low power)" with direct `[Disable Auto-detect]` button in notification shade.
3. **Zero-CPU Standby Gating**: In standby (no active trip, no live sharing), `LiveTrackingService` stops collecting `locationManager.getLocationUpdates()` (`stopLocationTracking()`), releases wake lock (0 mW), and powers down GPS completely. Process stays alive at FGS priority (`adj 200`), completely immune to background kills and cached limits.
4. **Direct Motion Wake Burst**: When `Sensor.TYPE_SIGNIFICANT_MOTION` fires in pocket, `MotionWakeManager` calls `AppStateManager.handleMotionWake()` directly. Since `LiveTrackingService` is already running as a valid Location FGS, GPS updates are delivered without restriction and the confirmation burst evaluates locomotion cleanly.
5. **Clean Option A Shutdown in MANUAL Mode**: When switched to `TripMode.MANUAL` or when tapping `[Disable Auto-detect]`, `LiveTrackingService` stops completely, notification is removed, and hardware sensor is disarmed.

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **FT21-01** | **Restore Persistent Notification & Fix Screen-Off Auto-Start**: Phone in pocket riding bike didn't auto-start. Identified missing persistent notification as the root cause. | `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt`, `MotionWakeManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `AndroidManifest.xml`, `StandbyFgsAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Restored persistent standby FGS notification launched from foreground. Implemented dynamic location updates gating (zero CPU/GPS in standby). Eliminated crashed `MotionWakeBroadcastReceiver`. Added 14 unit tests in `StandbyFgsAutoStartTest.kt`. All 169 unit tests pass, `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`. |

## 34. Round 31: Robust Viewport Fit & Screen Orientation Projection Overhaul (2026-10-07)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **MAP31-01** | **Trip Polyline Fit to Screen Root Cause & Overhaul**: When fitting trip polylines to screen (e.g. auto-selected finished trip or tapping "Fit to track"), the polyline extended off-screen under the top LocalityCard and was improperly positioned under Course-Up rotation. **Root Causes**: (1) Screen-aligned projection formula in `OsmMapView.kt` had inverted signs for tangent projection $\Delta North$ ($-\Delta North$ vs $+\Delta North$), which mirrored screen axes under Course-Up rotation ($\alpha = -65^\circ$). (2) Center un-rotation $midY$ had inverted sign, shifting camera in the opposite geographic latitude direction. (3) Bottom inset did not account for the floating `Recenter` pill ($\ge 186\text{dp}$ clearance when recording, $\ge 130\text{dp}$ clearance normally), causing lower points to render behind buttons. (4) Zoom calculation lacked safety margins, fitting track to 100% of the viewport with 0 padding buffer. (5) Asynchronous `animateTo(cameraCenter)` conflicted with OSMDroid layout and zoom changes. **Solution**: Rewrote `fitPointsToUnobstructedViewport` and `calculateOpticalCenter` with closed-form screen rotation mathematics ($u = \Delta E \cos\alpha + \Delta N \sin\alpha$, $v = \Delta E \sin\alpha - \Delta N \cos\alpha$), added guaranteed Recenter pill clearance, added a 12% safety padding buffer, and applied synchronous `map.controller.setCenter(cameraCenter)` + `stopAnimation(false)`. | `OsmMapView.kt`, `ViewportFitTest.kt`, `app/build.gradle.kts` | **Completed** | Validated against real Trip 161 coordinates (22.18 km driving trip, 1,885 points) across all rotations (0°, -65°, 45°, 90°, 180°, 270°). All 1,885 points strictly project within the unobstructed aperture. Unit tests in `ViewportFitTest.kt` pass (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`). |
| **VER-06** | **Build Gate & Version Increment**: Increment versionCode to 46 and versionName to "1.4.8". Verify all unit tests pass. | `app/build.gradle.kts` | **Completed** | `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest assembleDebug` passed (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`, all unit tests passed). |

## 36. Round 33: Android Auto IPC Timeout & Main-Thread Geocoding Deadlock Fix (2026-10-08)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **AA33-01** | **Android Auto App Launch Hang & Freeze at 4.7 km/h**: User reported that opening the Android Auto app at the start of the trip displayed speed 4.7 km/h and hung for several minutes until force-stopped and restarted. **Root Cause**: `AutoMediaService.onLoadChildren` ran synchronously on the Android Main (UI) thread and called `locationManager.resolveMultiLanguageData(lat, lng)`. On cache miss, `resolveMultiLanguageData` executed 4 blocking Google Geocoder IPC calls, Nominatim reverse geocode (`Thread.sleep(1000L - elapsed)` + 6s HTTP timeout), and OSRM nearest street match (`@Synchronized` lock + `Thread.sleep(1100L - elapsed)` + 4s HTTP timeout) directly on the Main Looper. This caused Android Auto's Binder IPC to time out, freezing both the car screen (stuck at initial rolling speed 4.7 km/h) and the phone UI. **Solution**: (1) Added `allowNetwork` parameter and strict Main Thread Guard in `LocationManager.resolveMultiLanguageData` that returns immediately from memory/disk cache/lastGood without ever performing network I/O or `Thread.sleep` on the Main Looper, scheduling background refresh on `ioScope` if needed. (2) Overhauled `AutoMediaService.onLoadChildren` to immediately decouple Binder IPC via `result.detach()`, run child item generation on `Dispatchers.IO` with `allowNetwork = false`, and safely catch exceptions with `result.sendResult()`. (3) In `LocationManager.masterLocationFlow`, passed `allowNetwork = false` for initial snapshot resolution so collector startup never stalls. (4) Added `MediaSessionCompat.Callback` to `AutoMediaService` to properly handle Android Auto playback commands. | `AutoMediaService.kt`, `LocationManager.kt`, `AutoMediaServiceAndMainThreadSafetyTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with unit tests in `AutoMediaServiceAndMainThreadSafetyTest.kt` (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`). ## 37. Round 34: Viewport Scale Factor Dynamic Fit, Roundabout Transition Release & Polish Hamlet Territorial Resolution (2026-10-08)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **MAP34-01** | **Fit to Screen Track Clipping under Profile Font Scaling (Trip #162 Clipping Left Track)**: Clicking "Fit to screen" during a driving trip clipped the left portion of the polyline. **Root Cause**: The user's CAR profile configured `profile_CAR_font_scale = MAXIMUM` (`scaleFactor = 1.75f`). In OSMDroid, `map.tilesScaleFactor = 1.75f` magnifies the tile ground resolution by $1.75\times$ (tile size $256 \times 1.75 = 448\text{px}$). However, `fitPointsToUnobstructedViewport` and `calculateOpticalCenter` hardcoded standard tile size 256 resolution ($156543.03392\text{ m/px}$), ignoring `map.tilesScaleFactor`. As a result, the map was rendered at 175% magnification, blowing $\sim 412\text{px}$ off the screen boundaries. **Solution**: (1) Adjusted ground resolution at equator dynamically by `156543.03392 / map.tilesScaleFactor` in both `fitPointsToUnobstructedViewport` and `calculateOpticalCenter`. (2) Adjusted camera ground offset using `actualMpp = equatorRes * cosMeanLat / 2^calculatedZoom`. (3) Expanded safety margin from 12% to 15% (0.85 safe width/height). | `OsmMapView.kt`, `ViewportFitTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `ViewportFitTest.testMaximumFontScaleTilesScaleFactorFitsWithoutClipping` verifying 100% of points inside unobstructed viewport under 1.75x tile scale factor. All tests pass (`BUILD SUCCESSFUL`). |
| **STREET34-01** | **Street Name Delay at Roundabout (Rondo NSZZ Solidarność -> Fabryczna)**: After exiting the roundabout onto Fabryczna, the street display remained hung on "Rondo NSZZ Solidarność" for ~400m / 20 seconds. **Root Causes**: (1) In `LocationManager.resolvePlace`, a rogue legacy check suppressed any non-major OSM street when it did not match `trajectoryStreet`, forcing the old street unconditionally when OSRM was rate-limited or unavailable. (2) Inside roundabouts, heading changes are incremental ($10^\circ-15^\circ$ per fix), so the single-step kinematic turn detector ($\Delta\text{bearing} \ge 30^\circ$) did not trigger upon exit. (3) Roundabouts were treated as persistent trajectory corridors rather than transient junction nodes. **Solution**: (1) Removed the rogue street suppression check from `resolvePlace`. (2) In `LocationManager.applyStreetHysteresis`, added explicit roundabout exit detection (`isRoundaboutExit`): when exiting a roundabout (`Rondo...`) onto an exit road, commit the exit street immediately on the 1st fix with 0s delay. | `LocationManager.kt`, `StreetResolutionTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with unit test `Exiting roundabout commits exit street immediately on first fix without lag` in `StreetResolutionTest.kt`. |
| **GEO34-01** | **Spurious Hamlet "Krzemionki" Usurping Locality "Kozy" (Trip #161)**: In Trip 161 route summary, "Krzemionki" appeared as a visited locality between Kozy visits. **Root Cause**: In OpenStreetMap Nominatim, "Krzemionki" is tagged as a `hamlet` (część wsi / przysiółek) belonging to `gmina Kozy`. In `LocationManager.geocodeWithOsm`, `villageOrTown` checked `str("village") ?: str("town") ?: str("hamlet")`. Because OSM had no village polygon covering that road, it selected `hamlet: "Krzemionki"`, usurping the genuine territorial gmina/locality `Kozy`. **Solution**: (1) In `LocationManager.geocodeWithOsm`, removed `hamlet` from `villageOrTown`, so in a Polish gmina, only genuine villages or towns can take precedence over `gminaName`. (2) In `SpatialCacheHelper.deserializePlaceInfo` and `migrateLegacyKeys()`, canonicalized hamlets `Krzemionki` and `Małe Kozy` in `gmina Kozy` to canonical locality `Kozy`. (3) In `TripDatabaseHelper.jsonToPlaces` and `onOpen()`, sanitized existing trip records to map `Krzemionki` in `gm. Kozy` to `Kozy` and collapse consecutive duplicate locality visits. | `LocationManager.kt`, `SpatialCacheHelper.kt`, `TripDatabaseHelper.kt`, `StreetResolutionTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with unit tests `OSM Nominatim hamlet in gmina Kozy does not usurp genuine town or gmina Kozy` and `OSM Nominatim real village in Polish gmina takes precedence over gmina name`. Trip 161 cleanly collapses to `Bielsko-Biała -> Kozy -> Bujaków -> Kobiernice -> Czaniec`. |

## 40. Round 37: Zero-Idle-Drain Overhaul & Live Sharing Stationary GNSS Shutdown (2026-10-09)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **BAT37-01** | **Live Sharing Alone Overnight Drain Root Cause & Stationary GNSS Shutdown**: Physical device diagnostics (`dumpsys batterystats`, `dumpsys location`) revealed that active Live Sharing without an active trip (`LIVE_ONLY` mode) kept high-accuracy GNSS hardware polling every 12 seconds for 7h 11m continuously overnight while motionless on a nightstand, burning 316.08 mAh (GNSS) and triggering 1,527 WiFi scans (1,142 mAh WiFi drain). **Root Cause**: `AppStateManager` disarmed `MotionWakeManager` and configured 12s GPS sampling in `LIVE_ONLY` regardless of motion. **Solution**: (1) Fixed mode resolution in `AppStateManager.recalculateState()` so `FOREGROUND_VIEW` takes precedence over `LIVE_ONLY` when screen is on. (2) Implemented `startLiveOnlyWatcher()` in `AppStateManager`: when in `LIVE_ONLY`, if the device is stationary for $\ge 30\text{s}$ (accelerometer variance $< 0.045$ or speed $< 0.35\text{ m/s}$), completely power down GPS via `LocationManager.stopLocationUpdates()`, arm ultra-low power hardware motion sensor (`Sensor.TYPE_SIGNIFICANT_MOTION`, ~0 mW), and switch to lightweight periodic presence heartbeat. (3) In `AppStateManager.handleMotionWake()`, significant physical motion re-engages GPS tracking seamlessly. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `LocationManager.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with automated unit tests in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` covering mode precedence, stationary gating, and motion wake re-engagement. Builds with 0 errors. |
| **BAT37-02** | **Notification Posting Wakelock Storm Elimination (678 Wakelocks Overnight)**: Diagnostics showed 678 wake locks from `NotificationManagerService:post` and 594 wakeup alarms overnight. **Root Cause**: `LiveTrackingService.updateNotification()` included `${liveSession.getFormattedRemaining()}` (`⏱️ 7h 12m elapsed`) in the notification body. Because the elapsed minute changed every 60s, `contentChanged = true`, triggering `manager.notify()` every minute. **Solution**: In `LiveTrackingService.updateNotification()`, when `activeTrip == null`, removed the ticking minute string and made the notification text static (`"Live Sharing Active • $place"`). `contentChanged` remains `false` when stationary, completely eliminating repetitive notification wake locks and system wakes. | `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.testLiveOnlyNotificationTextStabilityWithoutTrip`. Passes all unit tests. |
| **BAT37-03** | **LiveTrackingService Location Stream Decoupling & Zero-GPS Presence Heartbeat**: In `LiveTrackingService.evaluateServiceState()`, decoupled `hasLive` from `startLocationTracking()`. In `LIVE_ONLY` mode (no active trip), `stopLocationTracking()` is called, releasing the continuous `masterLocationFlow` subscription and freeing GNSS hardware. In `LiveSharingManager`, implemented `startStationaryHeartbeat()` which transmits a periodic 50ms HTTP presence snapshot (`speed = 0 km/h`, battery, cached place) every `syncIntervalMinutes` without turning on GPS hardware or appending breadcrumb points, keeping the web viewer "ONLINE" while device Doze is maintained. | `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with unit tests in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`. All 198+ unit tests pass. |

## 43. Round 40: Charging Thermal Mitigation, CacheManager Prefetch Storm Elimination & Live Sync Cadence Calibration (2026-10-09)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **THERM40-01** | **Elimination of Background CacheManager Prefetch Storm Triggered on Charger**: Device diagnostics revealed severe phone heating and battery drain when plugged into the car charger. Logcat revealed `NotificationService: Package enqueue rate is 6.5591984. Shedding 0|janush.tech.whereami|3001|null|10552`. **Root Cause**: `CacheManager` had `autoStartOnCharger = true` and `allowOnBattery = true`, so plugging in the phone automatically launched 3-pass prefetching across all 75 trips in the database, flooding `NotificationManager` on every point, issuing thousands of Nominatim/Overpass queries and SQLite writes concurrently with driving navigation. **Solution**: (1) Suppressed auto-prefetch in `startPrefetch()` and `fetchMissingBoundaries()` whenever an active trip is recording or Live Sharing is active (`TripManager.activeTrip != null || LiveSharingManager.currentSession?.isActive == true`). (2) Throttled `updateNotification()` in `CacheManager` so notification posts occur at most once every 2,500ms (or on pass completion/pause), eliminating notification rate-limit shedding and CPU spikes. | `CacheManager.kt` | **Completed** | Auto-prefetch blocked during recording/live sharing. Verified with zero notification flooding. Passes build gates. |
| **THERM40-02** | **Sustainable Live Sharing Sync Intervals & In-Flight Concurrency Mutex**: Driving Live Sync cadence when charging was previously set to 2.0s, causing ~24 HTTP POSTs/min over LTE/5G. This kept the cellular RF Power Amplifier at 100% duty cycle, preventing low-power C-DRX radio sleep and causing thermal throttling (`pixel-thermal: active throttling`). **Solution**: (1) Re-calibrated charging sync intervals to sustainable profiles allowing modem sleep: `CAR` at 5.0s, `CYCLING`/`MTB` at 6.0s, `RUNNING` at 8.0s, `HIKING`/`WALKING` at 10.0s. (2) Added `@Volatile private var isSyncInFlight = false` concurrency mutex in `LiveSharingManager.flushPointsToServer()` to eliminate overlapping concurrent HTTP requests over cellular data. | `TripModels.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `LiveShareChargingSyncTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with automated unit tests in `LiveShareChargingSyncTest.kt`. |
| **LIVE40-01** | **Initial Charging State Sticky Intent Initialization**: User reported that guest did not observe frequent updates when phone was plugged in. **Root Cause**: `AppStateManager._isCharging` defaulted to `false` and did not inspect `BatteryManager.isCharging` or the sticky intent returned by `registerReceiver(null, IntentFilter(ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED))` at app launch. If the phone was already plugged into the charger before opening the app, `_isCharging` remained `false` until an OS broadcast occurred, causing Live Sharing to fall back to the 1-minute battery interval. **Solution**: In `AppStateManager.registerBatteryReceiver()`, immediately query `BatteryManager.isCharging` and inspect the sticky `Intent` returned by `registerReceiver`. In `LiveSharingManager.isDeviceCharging()`, query `AppStateManager` and fallback directly to `BatteryManager.isCharging`. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `LiveSharingManager.kt` | **Completed** | Charging status is now guaranteed accurate immediately upon process launch. |
| **SRV40-01** | **Asynchronous Debounced Disk Persistence & Web Viewer Cadence Indicators**: Replaced blocking `fs.writeFileSync()` in `server/server.js` with debounced asynchronous `fs.writeFile()` (2-second trailing window) to prevent Node.js event-loop freezes on NAS spinning disks. In `server/public/index.html`, added explicit cadence indicators in the status badge (`⚡ LIVE (boost)` vs `ONLINE (1m)`) and telemetry line (`(⚡ ~5s)` vs `(1m)`), and calibrated polling interval to 2.5s. | `server/server.js`, `server/public/index.html` | **Completed** | Verified in server code and viewer polling loop. |

## 46. Round 43: Offline Cache Self-Management, Boundary Fetch 429 Resilience & Auto-Prefetch Automation (2026-10-09)

| **BOUND43-01** | **Fix 22 Missing Boundaries Fetch Breakdown (Rate Limiting, Cooldown Wait & International Country Handling)**: User reported 22 missing boundaries in Offline Data & Cache (`Screenshot_20261009-065652.png`), where tapping "Fetch" started downloading but aborted back to 22 missing. **Root Cause**: (1) `CacheManager` hardcoded `"pl"` for all places, causing Slovak localities (`Liptovský Mikuláš`, `Dolný Kubín`, `Ružomberok`, etc.) from past trips to query Poland in Nominatim and fail. (2) `BoundaryHelper.getLocalityBoundary` fired fallback queries sequentially with 0ms delay, triggering Nominatim HTTP 429. (3) On 429, `coolDownUntil` caused all subsequent cities in the loop to return `null` immediately in 1ms without caching to disk or waiting out the cooldown. (4) Junk place names (`Likwidacja`, `Serwis`, `Powiat`, `koło`) in spatial cache had no boundary in OSM. **Solution**: (1) Added `getDistinctBoundaryTargets()` in `CacheManager` extracting rich metadata (`name`, `countryCode`, `municipality`, `lat`, `lng`) and filtering junk names. (2) In `BoundaryHelper`, added `throttle()` enforcing $\ge 1500\text{ms}$ delay between ALL network queries. (3) If 429 cooldown is active, `BoundaryHelper` suspends with coroutine delay to wait it out instead of discarding the remaining queue. (4) Caches authoritative empty arrays (`[]`) to disk for non-polygon places so they resolve as completed (`hasBoundary == true`). | `BoundaryHelper.kt`, `CacheManager.kt`, `BoundaryRankingTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new automated test `testParseMultiPolygonGeoJson` in `BoundaryRankingTest.kt`. All unit tests pass. |
| **AUTO43-01** | **Autonomous Post-Trip & Foreground Cache Prefetch Trigger**: User noted auto-download settings (`autoStartOnWifi`, `autoStartOnCharger`) did not seem to work automatically. **Root Cause**: `calculateCacheDeficit()` was only ever called when opening `CacheManagerDialog` UI; no background or lifecycle triggers existed. **Solution**: (1) Wired `CacheManager.onTripFinished()` directly into `TripManager.stopTrip()` so completing a recording trip automatically calculates deficit and triggers prefetch if on Wi-Fi or charger (or posts notification). (2) Added `calculateCacheDeficit()` to `MainActivity.onStart()` so returning to the app refreshes cache deficits and evaluates auto-fetch. | `TripManager.kt`, `CacheManager.kt`, `MainActivity.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `TripManager.stopTrip()` and `MainActivity.onStart()`. |
| **CACHE43-01** | **Self-Managing Cache Engine & Legacy Junk Purge**: Purged junk city tokens (`Likwidacja`, `Serwis`, `Powiat`, `koło`) in `SpatialCacheHelper.sanitizeKnownMismatches(db)`. Formulated and implemented self-managing cache lifecycle strategy (fresh vs stale tiers, post-trip opportunistic refresh, negative caching, rate-limit resilience). | `SpatialCacheHelper.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in SQLite schema and database open hook. |

## 47. Round 44: Stale-While-Revalidate (SWR) 3-Tier Cache Read & User-Configurable 1GB Storage Budget (2026-10-09)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **CACHE44-01** | **Stale-While-Revalidate (SWR) 3-Tier Staleness Gates**: User requested enhancing cache read so entries older than 1 year are not trusted blindly, but never blank the screen when offline. **Solution**: In `LocationManager.kt`, implemented 3-tier staleness evaluation: (1) *Fresh (< 60 days)*: served immediately from SQLite with 0 network calls. (2) *Aging (60-365 days)*: served immediately from cache, enqueued to `pendingRefreshPoints` for post-trip routine on Wi-Fi + charger. (3) *Obsolete (> 365 days)*: if internet is available, SWR serves cached data immediately (0ms UI latency, no screen blink) while launching asynchronous background geocode refresh (`scheduleBackgroundRevalidation`) to overwrite SQLite with fresh timestamp; if offline, safely falls back to cached text and enqueues point for post-trip update. | `LocationManager.kt`, `SpatialCacheHelper.kt`, `SpatialCacheGridTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new automated tests in `SpatialCacheGridTest.kt` (`testCacheAgeStalenessGates`). All tests pass. |
| **CACHE44-02** | **Configurable Storage Budget & Post-Trip Stale Sweeper**: User requested configurable cache limit up to 1GB or more so cold cache is retained indefinitely unless disk space is needed. **Solution**: (1) Added `maxCacheSizeMb` (default 1024 MB / 1 GB) in `CacheManager` with interactive UI selector (500 MB, 1 GB, 2 GB, 5 GB) in `CacheManagerDialog.kt`. (2) In `SpatialCacheHelper.enforceStorageLimit()`, purges junk tokens, then trims entries $> 365$ days, then evicts via LRU only if total size exceeds user-configured limit. (3) In `CacheManager.onTripFinished()`, automatically refreshes queued stale corridor points and enforces storage limits on Wi-Fi / charger. | `CacheManager.kt`, `CacheManagerDialog.kt`, `SpatialCacheHelper.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `CacheManagerDialog` and `SpatialCacheHelper.enforceStorageLimit()`. |

| **SPEED46-01** | **Driving Speed 0.0 Clamping & Intersection Start Delay Fix**: User reported speed was undefined / 0.0 km/h and suddenly jumped to 30 km/h, unstable at low speed, with a big delay starting from an intersection. **Root Cause**: `LocationManager.hybridSpeedUpdate` checked `StationaryDetector.isPhysicallyStationary` and clamped speed to `0.0 km/h` unless `rawSpeed > 9.7 m/s` (35 km/h) or `speedAccuracyMps < 0.4 m/s`. When stopping at an intersection, accelerometer variance dropped below threshold. When moving off smoothly at 5-30 km/h, speed was forcibly clamped to 0.0 km/h until exceeding 35 km/h! Also delayed auto-start. **Solution**: Bypassed accelerometer clamping whenever physical displacement occurs (`distMoved > 3.0f || cumulativeDistMoved > 6.0f`) or raw GPS reports positive speed (`rawSpeed > 1.2 m/s`). Lowered foreground standby polling interval from 10s to 3s with 20s dwell, eliminating intersection start delays. | `LocationManager.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt`, `SpeedKinematicsTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new automated tests in `SpeedKinematicsTest.kt` (`testStartingFromStopNotClampedByStationaryDetector`, `testTrueDeskRestClampedByStationaryDetector`). |
| **TRIP46-01** | **Trip Auto-Stop Watchdog Immunity Against Indoor Drift & Mall Walking**: User reported trip didn't auto-stop while walking in the mall or at home, continuing to record until stopped manually. **Root Cause**: In `TripManager.onLocationUpdate`, `distFromAnchor > 12.0` executed `lastMovingTimestamp = now` even when `isMoving == false` (speed < autoStopSpeedKmh). Walking in the mall or indoor multipath drift constantly exceeded 12m from the last anchor, repeatedly resetting the 5-minute auto-stop timer. **Solution**: Strictly guarded `lastMovingTimestamp = now` with `if (isMoving)`. In vehicle profiles, sub-threshold walking and indoor drift no longer reset the watchdog, allowing trips to auto-stop reliably after 5 minutes of non-driving. | `TripManager.kt`, `SpeedKinematicsTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new unit test `testIndoorDriftDoesNotResetAutoStopInVehicleProfile` in `SpeedKinematicsTest.kt`. |
| **STREET46-01** | **Major-to-Side Street Switch Latency on Turn (Krakowska $\to$ Skotnica)**: User reported turning from Krakowska into Skotnica street appeared with noticeable delay while continuing to show Krakowska. **Root Cause**: (1) Turn detection required `speedKmh >= 5f`; slowing down to turn at 2-4 km/h failed to register the turn timestamp. (2) `applyStreetHysteresis` required 5 candidate readings and 6s for city driving ($> 15\text{ km/h}$). (3) CAR profile `minSpeedForBearing` was 2.5 m/s (9 km/h). **Solution**: (1) Lowered turn detection speed gate to `speedKmh >= 3f`. (2) Lowered `minSpeedForBearing` for CAR to `1.2 m/s` (4.3 km/h). (3) Relaxed city driving hysteresis for major-to-side street switches to 3 confirmations / 3.5s. | `LocationManager.kt`, `StreetResolutionTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new automated unit test `Turning from major Krakowska to side street Skotnica commits promptly after 3 confirmations and 3500ms` in `StreetResolutionTest.kt`. |
| **SCREEN46-01** | **Screen Dimmed & Lock Prevention Diagnostic**: User noticed phone screen was dimmed and prevented from locking. **Root Cause**: Inspection of device preferences in `device_data_round_20261010_2307/shared_prefs/where_am_i_ui_prefs.xml` showed `<boolean name="pref_keep_screen_on" value="true" />`. In `MainActivity.kt`, this adds `WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON`, which explicitly instructs the Android OS to keep the screen powered on while WhereAmI is in the foreground (dimmed by OS timeout, but never locking). **Solution**: Explained root cause and pointed to the "Keep Screen On" eye icon button in the main bottom controls card to toggle off. | `MainActivity.kt`, device preferences audit | **Completed** | Conclusively verified in device `shared_prefs`. |
| **UI46-01** | **Google Maps Style Small Stationary Position Dot & Subtle Accuracy Circle**: User expected a small dot and subtle accuracy circle around the dot. **Root Cause**: `makeMarkerIcon` previously rendered a giant 32dp solid circle, which covered/distorted the dynamic accuracy circle whose min radius was only 10dp. Also, `hasHeading` showed arrow during trip recording even when stationary. **Solution**: (1) Refactored stationary marker to a crisp 16dp Google Maps blue (`#1A73E8`) dot with a 2dp white border and subtle elevation shadow. (2) Scaled `AccuracyCircleOverlay` min radius to 14dp with soft blue fill (`0x1A1A73E8`) and 1dp stroke, forming a distinct halo. (3) Gated `hasHeading` strictly by `isMoving && (effectiveBearing != null)`, displaying the stationary dot whenever stopped. | `OsmMapView.kt`, `AccuracyCircleOverlay.kt`, `AccuracyCircleTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by updated unit test in `AccuracyCircleTest.kt`. |

## 53. Round 50: Field Testing (2026-10-12) Overnight Sleep Lifecycle Resolution, GNSS Complete Shutdown & Single Notification Invariant
