| **SLEEP50-01** | **Overnight Sleep Lifecycle Resolution & GNSS Complete Shutdown**: User reported that despite turning on sleep mode overnight with Live Sharing active, Android reported WhereAmI as highest battery consumer (64%). **Root Cause**: In `AppStateManager.recalculateState()`, `liveActive -> AppLifecycleMode.LIVE_ONLY` took precedence over `_isStandbySleeping.value`. Consequently, entering sleep mode failed to transition to `AppLifecycleMode.IDLE`, keeping `liveOnlyWatcherJob` running which continuously re-armed GPS polling (`LocationManager.updateSamplingInterval(15000L, 8000L)`). **Solution**: Prioritized `_isStandbySleeping.value -> AppLifecycleMode.IDLE` in `recalculateState()`, ensuring `startLiveOnlyWatcher()` is canceled, `LocationManager.stopLocationUpdates()` completely powers off GNSS hardware, and motion sensors remain disarmed throughout the night. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `testAppLifecycleModeResolutionWhenStandbySleeping` in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`. |
| **NOTIF50-01** | **Single Notification Invariant & Sleep State Decoupling**: User reported two notifications appeared simultaneously when entering sleep mode with Live Sharing active (`Where is Janusz - Live Sharing Active` with `[🌙 Sleep Now]` action alongside `🌙 WhereAmI Live Sharing Sleeping`). **Root Cause**: `LiveTrackingService` maintained `currentSession` and `tripMode` flow collectors in `startTracking()` that called `evaluateServiceState()`. When `ACTION_STANDBY_SLEEP` posted `NOTIF_SLEEP_ID = 3`, the background collectors or foreground restart re-evaluated `hasLive == true` (since `liveSession.isActive` remained true) and posted `NOTIF_ID = 2` without checking `isStandbySleeping`. **Solution**: (1) Updated `evaluateServiceState()` to explicitly check `AppStateManager.isStandbySleeping`. (2) In sleep mode, cancel `NOTIF_ID` (`notifManager.cancel(NOTIF_ID)`) and only post the sleep notification (`NOTIF_SLEEP_ID`). (3) Added `isStandbySleeping` flow collection in `LiveTrackingService.startTracking()` so the service immediately transitions to sleep hibernation and stays stopped without dual notifications. | `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `LiveTrackingService.kt`. |
| **SYNC50-01** | **Live Sharing Overnight Sleep Presence Cadence & Guest Viewer Status**: User noted that guest viewer showed updates every minute (`Last seen: 59s ago (1m)`) even though the user activated sleep mode. **Root Cause**: In `LiveSharingManager.startStationaryHeartbeat()`, interval was hardcoded to `session.syncIntervalMinutes * 60_000L` (1 minute from prefs). Cellular/Wi-Fi radio woke up every 60 seconds to ping the server. **Solution**: (1) In `startStationaryHeartbeat()`, when `isStandbySleeping` is true, relaxed heartbeat cadence to at least 15 minutes (`baseIntervalMs.coerceAtLeast(15 * 60_000L)`). (2) Added `isSleeping: Boolean` to live sync current payload. (3) Updated server guest viewer (`index.html`) to display `🌙 SLEEPING` badge, informational resting alert, and `🌙 ~15m` cadence text, preventing "DISCONNECTED" alerts during overnight sleep. | `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `server/public/index.html`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `testOvernightSleepPresenceCadenceRelaxation` in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`. |

On process death, `restoreUnclosedTripIfAny()` closed paused trips after `autoStopMinutes` (5m for CAR) as "CLOSED_ZOMBIE". **Solution**: (1) Persist pause state, start time, coordinates, and distance into SharedPreferences (`KEY_IS_PAUSED`, etc.) upon `pauseTrip()`. (2) `restoreUnclosedTripIfAny()` preserves paused trips regardless of stationary time and restores `activePause` + `_isPaused = true`. (3) Watchdog ignores stationary auto-stop while paused. ## 57. Round 54: Sleep Mode Notification Unification, Zero-CPU Standby In-Place Updates & Accidental Wake Prevention (2026-10-12)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **NOTIF54-01** | **Strict Single Notification Invariant & Zero-CPU In-Place Updates**: User reported that clicking "Sleep Now" caused notification to disappear briefly, then two conflicting notifications appeared in the tray (one with `[🌙 Sleep Now]` and one with `[▶ Activate]`). **Root Cause**: `LiveTrackingService.onStartCommand()` previously handled `ACTION_STANDBY_SLEEP` by stopping foreground service and posting a separate notification with `NOTIF_SLEEP_ID = 3`, without calling `AppStateManager.enterStandbySleep()`. Because `isStandbySleeping` remained false, `ensureAutoStandbyServiceRunning()` or background collectors restarted `LiveTrackingService` as FGS with `NOTIF_ID = 2`, leaving both notifications in the tray simultaneously. **Solution**: (1) Eliminated `NOTIF_SLEEP_ID = 3` and unified all notifications to `NOTIF_ID = 2`. (2) In sleep mode, `LiveTrackingService` remains running as a Zero-CPU FGS with location tracking and GPS shut down, updating notification in-place. (3) Handled `ACTION_STANDBY_SLEEP` by explicitly calling `AppStateManager.enterStandbySleep()`, disarming motion sensors and preserving sleep state. | `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` (`testSingleNotificationInvariantAndSleepIdUnification`). |
| **INTENT54-01** | **Notification Tap Clean Navigation & Drawer Decoupling**: User reported that clicking the sleeping notification unexpectedly opened the Live Location Sharing configuration bottom sheet (`ACTION_OPEN_LIVE_SHARING`). **Root Cause**: Persistent notification `contentIntent` hardcoded `ACTION_OPEN_LIVE_SHARING` whenever live sharing was active. **Solution**: Removed `ACTION_OPEN_LIVE_SHARING` from persistent notification `contentIntent` so tapping the notification opens `MainActivity` cleanly to the map/home screen without popping up configuration sheets. | `LiveTrackingService.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `LiveTrackingService.kt`. |
| **SLEEP54-01** | **Persistent Sleep Mode Across App Foreground & Screen Re-opens**: User reported that clicking the notification or opening the app immediately cancelled sleeping mode. **Root Cause**: `AppStateManager.setAppForegroundState(true)` unconditionally called `wakeStandbySleep()` whenever the app came to foreground. **Solution**: Removed auto-wake on foreground transition. Sleep mode is strictly persistent until the user explicitly taps `[▶ Activate]` on the notification or `[▶ Wake]` on the HUD bar. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` (`testSleepPersistenceAcrossForegroundTransitions`). |

## 55. Round 52: Web Companion Viewer Page Visibility API Gating & Sleep Polling Backoff (2026-10-12)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **WEB52-01** | **Web Live Sharing Page Visibility Gating & Zero-Background Polling**: Software analyzing battery usage reported Brave browser background Wi-Fi packet wakeups (`2.38 mAh`) due to open Live Sharing tabs maintaining network sockets during screen-off doze. **Solution**: (1) Hooked into the HTML5 Page Visibility API (`document.visibilityState` / `visibilitychange`). When tab is hidden or screen is off (`document.hidden == true`), the polling loop is completely paused, stopping all network fetch requests and background socket keepalives. (2) When returning to the tab / unlocking device, immediately triggers an instant `fetchTrack()` to refresh the map and seamlessly resumes polling. (3) When host is reported in sleep mode (`isHostSleeping == true`), backs off polling cadence from 4s to 30s. | `server/public/index.html` | **Completed** | Verified syntax and event listener integration. |

## 58. Round 55: Overnight Sleep Zero-CPU Hardening, Battery Broadcast Debounce & Notification Wakelock Elimination (2026-10-13)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: |
| **BAT55-01** | **Battery Receiver State-Change Debounce & 60m Sleep Heartbeat Preservation**: Overnight `batterystats` analysis (`12h 55m`, `4.56 mAh` total app drain = `0.09%` of battery) revealed `ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED` fired ~145 times on 1% battery drops/temp shifts, calling `recalculateState()` unconditionally. In `IDLE` mode (`isStandbySleeping = true`), `applyModeToLocationEngine()` cancelled `stationaryHeartbeatJob` and re-sent `startService(ACTION_STANDBY_SLEEP)` (`lastStartId=145`), which restarted the 60-minute sleep heartbeat timer from zero before it could ever reach 60m. **Solution**: (1) Filter `ACTION_BATTERY_CHANGED` via `shouldRecalculateOnChargingChange()` so `recalculateState()` runs ONLY when `_isCharging.value` actually transitions. (2) Preserve `stationaryHeartbeatJob` in `applyModeToLocationEngine()` when `shouldKeepSleepHeartbeatAlive()` is true (`IDLE && isStandbySleeping && hasLive`). (3) Send `ACTION_STANDBY_SLEEP` / `ACTION_STOP` only on mode transition (`oldMode != IDLE`). | `AppStateManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `testBatteryBroadcastDebounceAndSleepHeartbeatPreservation` in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`. |
| **BAT55-02** | **Notification State Signature Deduplication (Zero Notification Post Wakelocks)**: Overnight `batterystats` recorded `48` acquisitions of `NotificationManagerService:post:janush.tech.whereami` partial wakelock (`6s 109ms`) because `updateNotification(force = true)` bypassed content equality checks and called `manager.notify()` with identical content. **Solution**: Added `LiveTrackingService.computeNotificationSignature()` covering title, text, and all action state booleans; `updateNotification()` skips `manager.notify()` whenever `signature == lastNotificationSignature`, even when `force = true`. | `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `testNotificationSignatureDeduplicationPreventsRedundantWakelocks` in `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt`. |
| **BAT55-03** | **Screen-Off Suspension of Map 350ms Frame Loop & Compose UI Tickers**: Overnight `batterystats` recorded `3m 55s` of screen-off CPU time (`2.75 mAh`) caused by Compose `LaunchedEffect` loops (`OsmMapView` 350ms interpolation loop, `LocalityCard` 5s live timer loop, `Standby HUD` 60fps `rememberInfiniteTransition`, and `TripManager` 15s `startWatchdog` loop) continuing to tick while `MainActivity` was stopped in the backstack and CPU was awake. **Solution**: (1) Exposed `isAppInForegroundFlow` in `AppStateManager` and gated `OsmMapView` frame loop, `LocalityCard` ticker, Recording HUD/Toolbar tickers, and Standby HUD `rememberInfiniteTransition` so all UI loops suspend immediately on `MainActivity.onStop()`. (2) Scoped `TripManager.startWatchdog()` strictly to active trips (`_activeTrip.value != null`) and `AppStateManager.startStandbyInactivityWatcher()` strictly to awake `TripMode.AUTO` screen-off standby. | `OsmMapView.kt`, `MainActivity.kt`, `TripManager.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt` | **Completed** | Verified via unit tests and build gate. |
| **VER-28** | **Build Gate & Version Increment**: Increment `versionCode` to 69 and `versionName` to "1.6.9". Pre-completion build gate `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest assembleDebug` passed with `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`. | `app/build.gradle.kts` | **Completed** | `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest assembleDebug` passed. |

## 60. Round 57: AppStateManager & AutoSleepManager Circular Initialization Fix (2026-10-13)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: |
| **CRASH57-01** | **Resolve Circular Singleton Initialization StackOverflowError**: User reported app crashed on startup ("Where Am I keeps stopping"). **Root Cause**: `AppStateManager.<init>` called `AutoSleepManager.getInstance().evaluateAutoSleep()`, which in turn invoked `AppStateManager.getInstance()`. Because `AppStateManager.INSTANCE` was only assigned via `.also { INSTANCE = it }` after constructor exit, the recursive call spawned another `AppStateManager`, causing infinite mutual recursion and `StackOverflowError` in AndroidRuntime. **Solution**: (1) Assigned `INSTANCE = this` immediately in `AppStateManager.init` and `AutoSleepManager.init`. (2) Removed `evaluateAutoSleep()` from `AppStateManager.init`, letting it evaluate safely on lifecycle and screen-off events. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `AutoSleepManager.kt` | **Completed** | Verified logcat crash trace eliminated, builds and runs cleanly. |

| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **SLEEP62-01** | **Daytime Sleep Mode Strict Prevention**: Field testing showed that DND active in the morning triggered sleep mode because `AutoSleepManager.isEveningOrNightTime()` used `hour < 10`. Furthermore, 90-minute stationary standby hibernation in `AppStateManager` engaged 24/7 without checking whether it was daytime. **Solution**: (1) Replaced blanket morning hour check with `isNightOrBedtimeTime()`, restricting sleep triggers strictly to bedtime window and 1 hour before scheduled start. (2) Daytime DND unconditionally returns `false` in `shouldAutoSleep()`. (3) In `AppStateManager`, 90-minute standby hibernation is strictly gated by `isCurrentTimeInBedtimeWindow()`. | `AutoSleepManager.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt` | **Completed** | Verified via `AutoSleepManagerTest.kt` and `StickyManualPauseAndAutoResumeTest.kt`. |
| **TRIP62-02** | **Sticky Manual Pause Precedence Over Auto-Stop**: When a user manually pauses a trip and moves temporarily (e.g. walking around a rest area), the app temporarily auto-resumes to capture movement into the trip track. However, if the user becomes stationary before reaching the permanent continuation threshold, the trip must return to Pause instead of auto-stopping. **Solution**: (1) Added `KEY_MANUAL_PAUSE_STICKY` persisted across process restarts. (2) When stationary timeout is reached with `isManualPauseSticky == true`, calls `pauseTrip(isManual = false)` instead of `stopTrip()`. (3) Permanent continuation threshold (Car: $\ge 400\text{m}$ or $\ge 20\text{ km/h}$ for 45s; Cycling: $\ge 250\text{m}$; Hiking: $\ge 150\text{m}$) or explicit `resumeTrip()` clears sticky pause. (4) Filtered instantaneous GPS jitter from triggering auto-resume. | `TripManager.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with `StickyManualPauseAndAutoResumeTest.kt`. |

## 68. Round 65: Polyline Drawing Race Repair, C0 Position Interpolator Continuity & Profile Commit Thresholds (2026-10-14)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **TRACK65-01** | **Live Polyline Drawing Race Condition Repair (Straight Line & Chord Removal)**: Field test showed straight chord lines cutting across blocks from origin to current position and blinking real track. **Root Cause**: `OsmMapView.kt:770-783` modified `line.setPoints(livePoints)` at 35 FPS using stale `trackPoints` captured in Compose scope, clobbering `LaunchedEffect(trackPoints)`. When trip started with 1 point, `livePoints` was `[origin, gp]`, drawing a straight line across all city blocks. **Solution**: Removed live polyline tip modification from the 35 FPS frame loop in `OsmMapView.kt`, restoring `LaunchedEffect(trackPoints)` as the thread-safe single source of truth for polyline rendering. | `OsmMapView.kt` | **Completed** | Correlated with device logs, telemetry database, and screenshots (`Screenshot_20261014-193758.png` through `195613.png`). Verified in `OsmMapView.kt`. |
| **TRACK65-02** | **Continuous C0 Position Interpolation (Zero Step Discontinuity & Backward Rubber-Banding)**: User reported position icon moving backwards and jumping forward. **Root Cause**: In `PositionInterpolator`, `blendTargetLat/Lng` did not project for vehicle motion during `blendDurationMs`, causing a sudden step discontinuity of `speed * blendDurationMs` (up to 4-6m cliff) at the transition between blend and forward extrapolation. Furthermore, `onNewFix` sampled `calculateExtrapolatedPosition(timestamp)` rather than actual rendered position `interpolate(timestamp)`. **Solution**: (1) Updated `onNewFix` to sample `interpolate(timestamp)` as blend start. (2) Projected `blendTarget` to `timestamp + blendDurationMs`, ensuring $C^0$ continuity with zero step cliff into forward extrapolation. | `PositionInterpolator.kt`, `PositionInterpolatorTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new automated unit test `transition across blend boundary is continuous with zero jump` in `PositionInterpolatorTest.kt`. |
| **TRACK65-03** | **Profile-Specific Point Density (Restore High-Resolution Sidewalk & Road Curves)**: Field test showed angular, blocky polyline around corners. **Root Cause**: Commit threshold was set to `8.0m` for speeds $< 15\text{ km/h}$, yielding points only every 10–13m during pedestrian walking, cutting sharp 90° sidewalk corners. **Solution**: Profile-specific commit thresholds: `3.0m` for Walking/Hiking, `4.0m` for Running/Cycling/MTB, `5.0m` for Car. High-fidelity corner curves restored. | `TripManager.kt`, `SpeedKinematicsTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by new unit test `testProfileBasedCommitThresholds` in `SpeedKinematicsTest.kt`. |

## 77. Round 74: Live Sharing Overnight Sleep Auto-Entry & Stationary Screen-Off GPS Shutdown (2026-10-15)

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **BAT74-01** | **Live Sharing Overnight Sleep Auto-Entry**: Battery audit showed 12.1 mAh background consumption, 13 minutes active GNSS lock in screen-off Doze state, and 4 hours service runtime. **Root Cause**: `AppStateManager.updateStandbyInactivityWatcherState()` and `startStandbyInactivityWatcher()` had `!hasLive` gate, which stopped the background inactivity watcher whenever Live Sharing was active. This completely prevented `AutoSleepManager.evaluateAutoSleep()` and 90-minute standby hibernation from ever running in the background when Live Sharing was on. Furthermore, when Trip 208 ended at 23:39, neither `TripManager.stopTrip()` nor `monitorSubsystems()` triggered `evaluateAutoSleep()`, leaving the app in `LIVE_ONLY` mode polling GPS every 60s all night. **Solution**: (1) Removed `!hasLive` blocker from `updateStandbyInactivityWatcherState()` and `startStandbyInactivityWatcher()`, allowing the inactivity watcher and sleep evaluator to run for `(isAuto \|\| hasLive)`. (2) In `startLiveOnlyWatcher()`, immediately evaluate `AutoSleepManager.evaluateAutoSleep()` upon verifying stationary dwell. (3) Added `AppStateManager.onTripEnded()` called on trip finish in `TripManager.stopTrip()` to immediately trigger sleep evaluation. (4) In `AppStateManager.monitorSubsystems()`, evaluate `updateStandbyInactivityWatcherState()` and `AutoSleepManager.evaluateAutoSleep()` on active trip and live session changes. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `TripManager.kt`, `AutoSleepManagerTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with unit tests (`testLiveSharingEligibleForOvernightSleepWhenStationary`, `testStandbyInactivityWatcherActiveForLiveSharing`). |
| **BAT74-02** | **Stationary Screen-Off GPS Shutdown in `LIVE_ONLY` Mode**: In `AppStateManager.startLiveOnlyWatcher()`, when stationary for $\ge 30\text{s}$ with screen off on battery, polling GPS every 60s kept the GNSS hardware waking up and acquiring satellite locks 60 times an hour (~13 minutes over 4 hours) and prevented sustained Android Doze. **Solution**: When verified stationary for $\ge 30\text{s}$ while screen is off and unplugged, power down GPS hardware completely via `LocationManager.stopLocationUpdates()`. `MotionWakeManager`'s hardware `Sensor.TYPE_SIGNIFICANT_MOTION` (~0 mW) remains armed and immediately wakes and restores moving GPS cadence (15s / 6s) upon physical locomotion. Live Sharing presence continues transmitting via periodic HTTP heartbeats using the cached coordinates. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | **Completed** | Verified with unit tests (`testLiveOnlyStationaryGpsShutdownOnBatteryWhenScreenOff`). |
| **BAT74-03** | **Bedtime Window Boundary Alarms (`AlarmManager`)**: In `AutoSleepManager`, added exact/allow-while-idle alarm scheduling (`computeNextBoundaryTriggerMillis`, `scheduleBoundaryAlarm`) for `startTime` (22:30) and `endTime` (06:30). Guarantees that even if Android Doze suspends coroutines, the device wakes up at the exact bedtime boundary to enter or exit sleep mode. | `AutoSleepManager.kt`, `AutoSleepManagerTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by unit tests (`testComputeNextBoundaryTriggerMillisWhenAwake`, `testComputeNextBoundaryTriggerMillisWhenSleeping`). |

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: |
| **SLEEP75-01** | **Sleep/Standby Rapid Ping-Pong Oscillation Resolution**: User reported that in the morning, notifications cycled rapidly back and forth between "Sleeping" and "Standby", and opening the app showed the UI oscillating rapidly between Sleep and Standby modes in an unstoppable loop, persisting even after force-stopping and reopening. **Root Causes**: (1) `AutoSleepManager.shouldAutoWake()` triggered early wake whenever `followDnd && !isDndActive && isMorning` (04:00 to 11:00 AM) without checking if DND was ever active during the sleep session. For any user not using DND, `!isDndActive` is always true, causing `shouldAutoWake()` to fight `shouldAutoSleep()` inside the bedtime window. (2) `AppStateManager.enterStandbySleep()` and `wakeStandbySleep()` dispatched `ACTION_STANDBY_SLEEP` and `ACTION_STANDBY_ACTIVATE` via `startService()` back to `LiveTrackingService`, which called `AppStateManager` back, forming a recursive event-loop storm in the service queue. **Solution**: (1) In `AutoSleepManager`, added `wasDndActiveInSession` tracking; early morning DND wake is strictly gated on DND having been previously active during the sleep session (`wasDndActiveInSession && !isDndActive && isMorning`). (2) If an early wake occurs inside the bedtime window (via alarm or DND toggle), set `wasManuallyWokenThisCycle = true` to suppress re-entering sleep for the remainder of the window. (3) Added `SLEEP_STATE_TRANSITION_DEBOUNCE_MS = 3_000L` to `AppStateManager` to drop rapid oscillations. (4) Removed redundant `context.startService()` calls from `AppStateManager` since `LiveTrackingService` already observes `isStandbySleeping` via StateFlow. | `AutoSleepManager.kt`, `AppStateManager.kt`, `LiveTrackingService.kt`, `AutoSleepManagerTest.kt` | **Completed** | Covered by `testNoPriorDndInMorningDoesNotWakeInsideBedtimeWindow`, `testNoSleepWakeOscillationInMorning`, and `testEarlyWakeSuppressesReenteringSleepInSameWindow` in `AutoSleepManagerTest.kt`. |
| **GNSS75-02** | **Zero-GNSS Drain During Sleep Mode**: Device battery audit reported WhereAmI held an active GNSS lock for 13 minutes overnight in screen-off state. **Root Cause**: `LiveSharingManager.startStationaryHeartbeat()` executed `LocationManager.getCurrentLocationSingle(DisplayLanguage.PL)` every 60 minutes during sleep mode with a 10s timeout. Because the request used `Priority.PRIORITY_HIGH_ACCURACY` with no cancellation token, indoors/in bed the GNSS chip remained powered up at maximum power searching for satellites. **Solution**: (1) Completely eliminated GNSS requests during sleep mode in `LiveSharingManager.startStationaryHeartbeat()`. Stationary presence heartbeats during sleep transmit using cached/last known coordinates. (2) In `LocationManager.getCurrentLocationSingle()`, passed a `CancellationTokenSource` linked to coroutine cancellation (`cont.invokeOnCancellation { cts.cancel() }`) to ensure any cancelled single-location request immediately halts GNSS hardware at the Google Play Services level. | `LiveSharingManager.kt`, `LocationManager.kt` | **Completed** | Verified in `LiveSharingManager.kt` and `LocationManager.kt`. |
| **VER-48** | **Build Gate & Version Increment**: Increment `versionCode` to 94 and `versionName` to "1.9.11". Pre-completion build gate `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest assembleDebug` passed with `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` (all 42 test suites passed, 0 failures). | `app/build.gradle.kts` | **Completed** | `./gradlew testDebugUnitTest assembleDebug` passed (0 test failures, 0 compilation errors). |

Evidence: five screen recordings and nine screenshots of build 114 (trip 234, CAR) aligned with `track_1792471636611_trip234.jsonl`, the trip and spatial-cache databases and the device prefs. Replay metrics are build 114 -> build 115 (`FieldReplayTest`, CAR).

The batch is now flushed before a request is changed or removed (`BatchedRequestSequencer`, latest change wins). (b) The map fed the render pipeline from the conflated Compose fix, so a batch arrived as its last state and the cursor interpolated one chord; it now reads every state from `TrackingRuntime.states`. (c) After missed frames or a batch, the pencil is refilled from the pipeline history (`pathBetween`) instead of chording, and dead-reckoned pencil points are dropped. (d) Vehicles keep their render latency through a data gap (dropping to 0 dead-reckoned into side roads, the 07:09:16 loop). | `LocationManager.kt`, `BatchedRequestSequencer.kt`, `tracking/TrackStateLog.kt`, `TrackingBridge.kt`, `RenderPipeline.kt`, `PencilTrail.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt` | Implemented, needs device check | `ScreenResumeRenderTest` (sequencer, state log, pathBetween, dropAfter, latency through gap) |

Prediction now integrates the filter's braking (Singer acceleration, stops at zero). | `TrackState.accelMps2`, `RenderPipeline.brakedTravel` | Implemented | stop backtrack p90 0.12 -> 0.05 m (20 stops); `ScreenResumeRenderTest` |

The large excursions are the post-gap cases of ENG85-01. | **PWR85-01** | **Sleep mode not woken by three morning alarms (bedtime 21:30-06:30, Follow DND, Sync with Morning Alarm).** `nextAlarmClock` reports only the upcoming alarm; when it rang, the clock app moved it to the snooze/next day and the code recorded that future time, so a fired alarm was never seen. With DND still on after 06:30 nothing woke the app. `AlarmFireTracker` treats the previous upcoming trigger as fired once it is in the past (persisted across restarts). | `AlarmFireTracker.kt`, `AutoSleepManager.kt` | Implemented | `AlarmFireTrackerTest` (snooze, three alarms, cancel, ringing, restart) |

## 112. Round 109: Inactive Visitors Dynamic Filtering & Marker/Altitude Z-Fighting Fix (2026-10-21, v1.18.13)

User feedback:
"I disabled the option (turned off) to show inactive visitors but they're still on the map. Additionally I'm seeing the alt badge and/or the visitor icon flickers, sometimes the Guest 1 badge is on top, sometimes it's below"

Root Cause Analysis:
1. **Inactive Visitors Not Filtered**: `server/server.js` computed inactivity threshold as `Math.max(5 * 60_000, syncInterval * 2 * 60_000)` (which equaled 10 minutes for default 5-min sync intervals). Even if visitor hadn't sent updates in 6 minutes (displayed as "6m ago" / "Inactive (> 5 min)"), server JSON returned `isInactive: false`. Client deserialization relied blindly on this server boolean.
2. **Marker & Alt Badge Flickering / Z-Fighting**: In `OsmMapView.kt`, `LaunchedEffect(visibleGuests, showGuestDistanceBadge, latLng)` re-ran on every GPS fix (1Hz–5Hz), clearing all guest markers and doing `map.overlays.add(gm)`, placing `gm` at the end of `map.overlays` (above `altitudeOverlay`). Within 16ms, `bringAltitudeOverlayToFront(map, altitudeOverlay)` in the 60fps frame loop detected that `altitudeOverlay` was not at the end, removing and appending it back to the end. The next GPS fix flipped it back, creating a relentless 1Hz–5Hz z-ordering war with continuous bitmap allocations and osmdroid overlay list churn.

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **ENG109-01** | Client-side autonomous 5-minute inactivity evaluation for live guests. | `LiveGuest.kt`, `server/server.js`, `LiveGuestTest.kt` | Implemented | Defined dynamic `val isInactive: Boolean get() = isInactiveAt(System.currentTimeMillis())` with 5-minute (`300_000ms`) threshold on client, decoupled from server sync interval; unified server threshold to 5 minutes |
| **ENG109-02** | Stable z-index placement and in-place marker reuse to eliminate flickering and z-fighting. | `OsmMapView.kt` | Implemented | Insert guest markers stably below host marker and altitude overlay (`insertGuestMarkerBelowHostOverlays`); reuse existing `Marker` instances and cache badge keys (`guestMarkerBadgeKeys`), eliminating overlay list churn, z-fighting, and per-fix bitmap allocations |
| **VER-109** | versionCode 144 / 1.18.13 | `build.gradle.kts`, `LiveGuestTest.kt` | Implemented | Unit tests passing; build verification complete |

## 113. Round 110: Instant Bulk Cache Deficit Discovery, Fetch Reaction & Map Progress Control (2026-10-22, v1.18.13)

User feedback:
"Today I opened the app and there was no map control related to the missing cache data to fetch on the screen, but when I opened the Offline Data & Cache I saw there are missing addresses to fetch - see screenshot. Then when I closed this panel and went back to the main screen the cache map controll appeared - see second screenshot. Fix discovering the missing data.
Also check if the fetching works well.
Check and fix the Fetch button reaction - now I click the button and nothing is happening, only after some time the fetching starts. I should see immediate reaction.
Also, while the fetching is in progress, turn the cache map controll into a control showing the fetching progress in kind of a progressing circle to see that it's working and what is the current status. Once finished it can stay with removed exclamation and replaced with green tick, once clicked show toast with a message that the data are downloaded (but check it first if in the meantime there are any new missing data)."

Root Cause Analysis:
1. **Slow Startup Deficit Discovery**: `calculateCacheDeficit()` iterated over every unique point (tens of thousands of trip points) executing individual `db.query(TABLE_CACHE, ...)` SQLite calls and JSON deserialization, blocking for 15–30 seconds before updating `_cacheDeficit.value`.
2. **Fetch Button Reaction Delay**: `startPrefetch()` and `fetchMissingBoundaries()` performed all candidate preparation inside the coroutine before setting `_prefetchState.value = PrefetchState.Running(...)`, keeping the UI in `Idle` state for several seconds after the click.

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **ENG110-01** | Bulk SQLite deficit discovery in single query (~10ms). | `SpatialCacheHelper.kt`, `CacheManager.kt`, `SpatialCacheGridTest.kt` | Implemented | Added `getAllCachedGridKeys()` to load all valid keys in one indexed scan; reduced discovery from ~20s to <10ms; cache control displays immediately on app open |
| **ENG110-02** | Instant Fetch button reaction and candidate indexing. | `CacheManager.kt` | Implemented | Immediately set `_prefetchState.value = PrefetchState.Running` on invocation; candidate generation leverages cached key sets for zero-latency start; added `calculateCacheDeficit()` on prefetch finish |
| **UI110-03** | Map Cache Control Live Progress Circle and Green Tick Completion. | `OsmMapView.kt`, `CacheManager.kt` | Implemented | Added `CircularProgressIndicator` ring around 42dp slate button with storage icon and percentage badge during prefetch; displayed green checkmark on completion; click re-verifies missing data, shows confirmation Toast, and dismisses button |
| **VER-110** | versionCode 144 / 1.18.13 | `build.gradle.kts`, `SpatialCacheGridTest.kt` | Implemented | Build and unit test verification passing |

## 116. Round 114: Screen-Off GNSS Drain, Heartbeat Loop & Fast-Track Stationary Shutdown Overhaul (2026-10-23, v1.18.17)

Evidence (`dumpsys batterystats`, window `2026-10-22 16:45:09` unplug at 100% to `2026-10-23 07:52:30` plug-in; 0 trips recorded, `gnss:fgs=9.72 mAh`, `gnss:bg=5.10 mAh`, total `20m 12s` screen-off GNSS):

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **PWR114-01** | `LiveSharingManager.currentSession` telemetry updates (`lastSyncTime`, `pendingPointsCount`, `viewCount`) from 5-min stationary heartbeats triggered `AppStateManager.recalculateState()`, restarting `startLiveOnlyWatcher()` and turning GPS back on for 30s every 5 min in screen-off. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `LiveTrackingService.kt` | Implemented | Added `isLiveSessionLifecycleChanged` and `.distinctUntilChanged` on `currentSession`; unit test `testLiveSessionTelemetryUpdatesDoNotTriggerLifecycleRecalculation` |
| **PWR114-02** | `LIVE_ONLY` false/indoor motion bursts ran `startLiveOnlyWatcher()` on exit instead of powering down GPS immediately, adding 30s extra GNSS per false burst and skipping the 60s-180s progressive cooldown. | `AppStateManager.kt` | Implemented | Added `shouldPowerDownGpsAfterLiveOnlyFalseBurst` and extended `scheduleMotionWakeRearm` to `LIVE_ONLY`; unit test `testLiveOnlyFalseBurstPowersDownGpsImmediatelyWhenScreenOff` |

## 117. Round 115: Trip 250 Polyline Chord Lock-Up Fix, Screen-Off Catch-Up Zigzag Elimination & Android Auto 1 Hz Real-Time Speed Updates (2026-10-23, v1.18.20)

Evidence (Trip 250 `2026-10-23 15:35:48` to `16:02:59`, `track_1792762549506_trip250.jsonl`, screenshots `Screenshot_20261023-153834.png` through `154424.png`):
- **Commit-path lock-up**: `TrackingEngine` accepted 1,223 fixes and `AdaptiveTrackSampler` emitted 502 road vertices, but `TripManager.onSmoothedPoints` committed only 23 vertices (`maxChord = 2,166.4m`) because the 1-point `TrackSpikeGuard.isLateralCourseOutlier` rejected the first vertex on the turn onto `Zagłębocze` (`seq 6`), freezing `recent = newPoints.takeLast(5)` on the pre-turn corridor and rejecting 683 consecutive vertices along the new road; additionally, stationary dwell at traffic lights did not advance `lastCommittedUtcMs` / sampler anchor timestamps.
- **Screen-on catch-up zigzag**: On screen wake (`15:38:30`), `RenderPipeline.onState(s)` accumulated a `~45m` backward correction offset `(oE, oN) = pre - post` across the 20s screen-off gap; `OsmMapView` refilled `pencilTrail` with `pathBetween` (`rawAt(t) = post`) and immediately appended `pose` (`post + oE = pre`), drawing a backward zigzag hairpin.
- **Android Auto screen-off speed freeze**: `AppStateManager.TRIP_RECORDING` set `maxDelay = SCREEN_OFF_BATCH_DELAY_MS` (`20_000L`) whenever `!isAppInForeground`, ignoring `isAutoMediaActive` / `UI_MODE_TYPE_CAR` and batching location delivery in 20s hardware bursts while driving with the phone screen off.

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **ENG115-01** | Eliminate straight-line chord lock-up (`1,972m` / `2,166m` chords) after turns and traffic lights. | `TripManager.kt`, `TrackSpikeGuard.kt`, `AdaptiveTrackSampler.kt`, `TrackSpikeGuardTest.kt`, `FieldReplayTest.kt` | Implemented | Removed 1-point `isLateralCourseOutlier` from `onSmoothedPoints` (retaining 3-point `isLateralVSpike(A, B, C)` with SQLite index sync), added 2-strike moving-point recovery rule, and refreshed stationary anchor/commit timestamps. Trip 250 replay: `committed` increased from `23` (`maxChord=2166.4m`) to `500/502` (`maxChord=50.6m`) |
| **ENG115-02** | Eliminate screen-on catch-up polyline zigzag behind cursor. | `RenderPipeline.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `RenderPipelineTest.kt` | Implemented | Increased `RenderPipeline.HISTORY_MS` to `35_000L`, cleared correction offsets across multi-second state gaps (`> 2,500ms`) and before `catchUp` frames in `OsmMapView`, hoisted `lastFedStateMs` across foreground toggles |
| **ENG115-03** | Real-time 1 Hz speed updates on Android Auto and during active trip recording when phone screen is off. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `AutoMediaService.kt`, `LocationManager.kt`, `BatteryOptimizationAndAutoStartTest.kt` | Implemented | Unbatched 1 Hz (`maxDelay = 0L`) in `TRIP_RECORDING` (batching reserved strictly for `BATTERY_SAVER` when not in car mode) and `ANDROID_AUTO` (`1000L, 1000L, 0L`); held refreshable `AutoMediaWakeLock` while `AutoMediaService` is active; processed all batched fixes chronologically in `LocationManager` |
| **VER-115** | versionCode 151 / 1.18.20 | `build.gradle.kts` | Implemented | `testDebugUnitTest` and `assembleDebug` passing |

## 118. Round 116: Battery & Modem Optimization, Car Auto-Start Duration, Live Share Resilience & UI Polish (2026-10-25, v1.18.21)

Evidence & Root Cause Diagnostics:
- **Modem & Wakelock Drain**: 500 mAh drain, 359 mAh modem state transitions, 32m 25s screen-off partial wakelocks. Screen-off 1 Hz geocoding (OSRM `/nearest` and Nominatim queries) and 10s ticking speed updates (`NotificationManager.notify()`) kept cellular radio awake and acquired CPU wakelocks.
- **Offline Sync Payloads**: In mountain valleys, failed Live Sharing requests accumulated multi-thousand-point JSON payloads uploaded repeatedly without backoff.
- **Car Auto-Start Failure**: 15s early abort killed car confirmation bursts while user was starting engine, triggering progressive cooldown backoff.

| Item ID | User Request / Description | Target Components / Files | Status | Verification & Evidence |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **PWR116-01** | Screen-off recording geocoding gate & single-flight request conflation. | `LocationManager.kt` | Implemented | Bypass OSRM `/nearest` on screen-off, throttle reverse geocode ($\ge 250$m or 60s), cancel in-flight geocode jobs |
| **PWR116-02** | Screen-off notification wakelock throttling. | `LiveTrackingService.kt` | Implemented | Omit fluctuating speed strings, throttle notification posts to 60s when screen is off |
| **PWR116-03** | Offline-resilient chunked Live Sharing & backoff. | `LiveSharingManager.kt` | Implemented | Capped queue batching to 40 points, added 10s-120s exponential backoff on HTTP failure |
| **AUTO116-04** | Car auto-start burst extension & profile-switch cooldown reset. | `AppStateManager.kt`, `TripManager.kt` | Implemented | Extended car burst min duration to 35s; reset consecutive false bursts on profile change |
| **DIAG116-05** | Persistent lifecycle diagnostics log. | `LifecycleDiagnosticsRecorder.kt` | Implemented | Appends JSONL events for bursts, trip transitions, and sleep modes |
| **MAP116-06** | Densify live vehicle pencil & custom track colors per activity profile. | `LiveTrackLine.kt`, `OsmMapView.kt`, `MapProfileConfigHelper.kt` | Implemented | Catmull-Rom densification for live vehicle pencil; customizable `trackColorHex` per profile |

## 119. Round 117: Overnight Sleep Extension to Morning Alarm & Motion Wake Deduplication (2026-10-26, v1.18.22)

Evidence & Field Diagnostics:
- **Overnight Oct 25-26 Drain**: App drain dropped 99.25% from 500 mAh to 3.74 mAh (0.4% of total device discharge). Zero GPS/wakelocks during 21:30-06:30 sleep window.
- **Morning Standby Burst**: Bedtime window ended at 06:30 while morning alarm was set to 07:30. At 06:43, phone movement on the nightstand triggered a false CAR confirmation burst (1m 42s GNSS, 21s wakelock, 1.20 mAh drain).
- **Duplicate Motion Wake Trigger**: `MotionWakeManager.onTrigger` called both `_motionEvents.tryEmit(Unit)` and `AppStateManager.handleMotionWake()` directly, executing redundant duplicate bursts 107ms apart.

## 120. Round 118: Overnight Sleep Burst Backoff, Avatar Deconfliction, Street Cache Sanitization, House Numbers & Smooth Tracking Recovery (2026-10-27, v1.18.24)

Evidence & Field Diagnostics:
- **Overnight Drain Diagnostics**: 48m GNSS active time overnight caused by rapid successive motion bursts when phone moved slightly during night; `computeBurstCooldownMs` remained flat (0s/60s). Screen-off stationary `LIVE_ONLY` mode kept GNSS active in certain states; `AutoSleepManager` kept `wasManuallyWokenThisCycle = true` indefinitely across days if not explicitly cleared.
- **Field Feedback Items (Oct 27)**:
  1. Guest avatar conflicted with altitude badge and saved place icons on map.
  2. Stationary at Home on Zielona street resolved falsely as Górska street due to corrupted spatial cache cells.
  3. House numbers failed to show while stationary at saved place with house number.
  4. Cache manager showed missing boundary cache data after download.
  5. Multipath drift indoors showed non-zero speed while cursor stayed still.
  6. Track color showed blueish instead of standard red for driving/walking and lacked activity profile color configuration.
  7. Curved polyline drawn with square corners / zigzags behind cursor tip.
  8. Side street `Graniczna` overrode main road `Krakowska (DK52)`.
  9. Walking trip polyline accuracy degraded from spike guard rejection deadlock.
